# Bestiary: Quest branches and quest foes

One card per enemy. Mechanics are Tier 1: read from the pinned code at `v4.0.0` and cited.
Tactics are derived from the mechanics and name the lines they rest on. Community notes are
forum and wiki claims graded against the code: Tier 1 confirmed, Tier F contradicted (struck),
Tier 3 not settled. The [bestiary index](index.md) has the cross-cutting rules, the one-line
tactic for every enemy and the tag vocabulary of `tactics/bestiary.json`.

## Enemies

- [Crystal guardian](#crystalguardian) (quest: Blacksmith crystal mine (branch 1 of depth 12-14); one per large mine room): Guardians are an obstacle, not a kill target: they cannot die and give nothing.
- [Crystal wisp](#crystalwisp) (quest: Blacksmith crystal mine (branch 1 of depth 12-14); the mine's regular spawn): Treat it as a turret you have to break line with. Step behind a crystal or let a guardian/ally stand in the line so it must drift adjacent, then melee it (30...
- [Crystal spire](#crystalspire) (quest boss: Blacksmith crystal mine (branch 1 of depth 12-14), centre of the giant mine room): Prepare first: find the guardians, note where they sleep, and mine a narrow tunnel to the spire (guardians crawl in enclosed cells).
- [Fungal core](#fungalcore) (quest boss: Blacksmith fungi mine - NOT reachable in v4.0.0 (quest type rolls only 1-2; fungi = 3 is unimplemented)): Unreachable in normal v4.0.0 play. If it ever appears: clear or avoid adjacent Fungal Sentries' lines, then beat on the core; it does not fight back.
- [Fungal sentry](#fungalsentry) (quest: Blacksmith fungi mine (centre of each large mine room when the quest type is FUNGI) - NOT reachable in v4.0.0 (fungi quest unimplemented)): Unreachable in normal v4.0.0 play. If present: route around its lines of fire; if it must die, approach along a path with no line until adjacent or attack...
- [Fungal spinner](#fungalspinner) (quest: Blacksmith fungi mine regular spawn - NOT reachable in v4.0.0 (fungi quest unimplemented)): Unreachable in normal v4.0.0 play. If present: pull it onto bare floor before trading blows, avoid stepping into its regrowth patches, and use ranged attacks...
- [Newborn fire elemental](#elemental-newbornfireelemental) (quest miniboss: Wandmaker ritual site (depth 7-9), summoned when all 4 ceremonial candles are placed): Light the candles standing next to the ritual centre so you can open with a surprise hit, ideally on or next to water.
- [Newborn fire elemental (ally)](#elemental-allynewbornelemental) (summoned ally from the Summon Elemental spell (default, un-imbued summon); not a naturally hostile spawn): Friendly summon: keep it between you and enemies; don't fight next to it on grass since its hits can ignite.
- [Wraith (corpse dust)](#dustwraith) (quest: Wandmaker corpse-dust quest (depth 7-9); spawns around the hero while the dust is carried, on any level): The moment you pick up the dust, walk straight back to the Wandmaker along explored corridors.
- [Greater elemental](#vaultbosselemental) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19); boss of the vault's final room): Bring both a stack of thrown weapons and a melee weapon (and wands if you have them).
- [Modified DM-100](#vaultdm100) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19)): Watch its patrol, come in from behind or the side, and open with a surprise hit; finish it before it gets a clear line.
- [Modified DM-200](#vaultdm200) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19)): Open with a surprise hit if you can, then fall back into a doorway or 1-wide corridor.
- [Fire elemental (vault)](#vaultelemental-fire) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19)): Surprise it from behind its patrol, ideally while you stand in or next to water; finish with frost/chill effects if you have them (each ~half its HP).
- [Frost elemental (vault)](#vaultelemental-frost) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19)): Surprise it on patrol and never fight it on water. A fire source (liquid flame, fire wand, burning grass) deals ~half its HP per application.
- [Shock elemental (vault)](#vaultelemental-shock) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19)): Surprise it on patrol; fight on dry floor with allies spread out. If blinded, back off out of its line until you can see again.
- [Dwarven ghoul (vault)](#vaultghoul) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19)): Surprise it on patrol and finish it before it trades many 16-22 hits. A solo vault ghoul dies for good; if you see two together, separate them or burst both...
- [Golem (vault)](#vaultgolem) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19)): Stealth around it using its patrol facing. If you must fight: surprise hit, then fight from a corridor with ranged attacks, and step back each time it pulls...
- [Marsupial rat (vault)](#vaultrat) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) - but no v4.0.0 code spawns it (only an unused import in VaultLongRoom)): Not spawned in v4.0.0. If present, one surprise hit or one normal hit kills it.
- [Captured shaman](#vaultshaman) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19)): Sneak up from behind its patrol and open with a surprise hit; kill it in the next turn or two.
- [Dwarven skeleton](#vaultskeleton) (quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19)): Surprise it on patrol, then either finish with a thrown weapon/wand from 2+ tiles or take the 6-12 burst (double DR) if finishing in melee.

## Rules these cards rely on

Tier 1, cited. Written for this region by the reader who verified its cards; the
[index](index.md#cross-cutting-rules) states the ones every region shares.

- **Accuracy vs evasion.** Every hit roll is acuRoll=Random.Float(attackSkill) vs defRoll=Random.Float(defenseSkill) (each scaled by Bless x1.25, Hex x0.8, Daze x0.5, champion factors); hit if acuRoll &gt;= defRoll. Magic attacks (hit(...,magic=true)) double the attacker's accuracy. If defense is 0 the defender's roll is 0, so the hit always lands. <small>`core/…/actors/Char.java:615`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:616`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:619`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:620`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:621`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:646`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:647`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:648`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:649`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:660`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:663`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:664`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:665`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:679`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:680`</small>
- **Surprise attacks.** A mob is surprised by the hero when the hero is invisible, or the mob has enemySeen=false, or the hero is outside the mob's field of view - and the hero's weapon can surprise-attack (not a Flail, not a weapon the hero lacks the strength for; unarmed/no weapon always can). A surprised (or paralysed) mob has defense 0, so the surprise hit is guaranteed; an invisible attacker also gets infinite accuracy. Sleeping mobs set enemySeen=false every turn; wandering mobs clear it while wandering. Dagger/Dirk/Assassin's Blade, Kunai and Throwing Knife deal extra damage on surprise; Rogue Preparation turns it into an assassination (Wound). A paralysed mob also clears enemySeen each turn, so paralysis doubles as a surprise-attack enabler. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:814`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:815`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:825`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:826`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:828`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1237`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:744`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:745`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:746`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:747`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:748`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:749`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:751`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:627`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:628`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:629`, `core/…/melee/Dagger.java:66`, `core/…/melee/Dagger.java:67`, `core/…/melee/Dagger.java:68`, `core/…/melee/Dagger.java:69`, `core/…/melee/Dagger.java:70`, `core/…/melee/Dagger.java:71`, `core/…/melee/Dirk.java:52`, `core/…/melee/AssassinsBlade.java:52`, `core/…/missiles/Kunai.java:52`, `core/…/missiles/ThrowingKnife.java:54`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:278`</small>
- **Detection and waking.** Sleeping mobs roll 1/(distance+stealth) per turn to notice a visible hostile (flying hostiles 2+ tiles away are undetectable to sleepers; Silent Steps also blocks); any negative buff wakes them. Wandering mobs roll 1/(distance/2+stealth). Taking damage wakes a sleeping mob to WANDERING and marks it alerted; wand/cleric-spell/armor-ability damage aggroes it straight onto the hero. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1189`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1190`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1191`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1192`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1201`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1206`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1207`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1210`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1211`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1212`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1213`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1217`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1218`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1227`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1244`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1245`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1248`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1249`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1250`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1251`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1252`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1270`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1282`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1283`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:902`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:904`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:905`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:906`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:909`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:910`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:912`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:913`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:914`</small>
- **Speed and attack delay.** Mob movement costs 1/speed() per step; base speed is 1 (same as the hero), Cripple halves speed, Haste triples it, Adrenaline doubles it, Stamina x1.5. A mob attack costs attackDelay() = 1 turn (Adrenaline /1.5). Enemies of normal speed therefore cannot be outrun without a speed edge; slower ones can. <small>`core/…/actors/Char.java:770`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:771`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:772`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:773`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:774`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:775`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:776`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:753`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:754`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:755`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:756`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1353`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1355`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1301`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1302`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:768`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:777`</small>
- **Reach and line of fire.** Default mob canAttack is adjacency only (plus champion reach). Bolt-type attacks use Ballistica.MAGIC_BOLT = STOP_CHARS\|STOP_SOLID: the bolt stops at the first character in the path (an ally, another enemy, a summon) and at the first solid cell (walls, closed doors, crystal). So any body or solid tile between you and a caster blocks the bolt and the caster must walk. PROJECTILE (hero thrown weapons) also stops at the target. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:558`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:559`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:560`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:562`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:563`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:42`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:43`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:44`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:45`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:47`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:117`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:118`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:119`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:120`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:121`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:122`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:123`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:124`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:125`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:130`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:131`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:134`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:137`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:138`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:140`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:141`</small>
- **Thrown weapon accuracy.** Hero thrown weapons get x1.5 accuracy when the target is not adjacent and x0.5 when adjacent (Point Blank talent raises the adjacent factor). Sniper subclass ignores DR with thrown weapons at range. Wand bolts (e.g. Magic Missile) do not roll accuracy at all: they damage directly. <small>`core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:215`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:216`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:218`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:223`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:224`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:225`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:226`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:228`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:231`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:394`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:395`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:396`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:397`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:56`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`</small>
- **Doors break line of sight.** A closed door is PASSABLE\|LOS_BLOCKING\|SOLID; an open door is just PASSABLE. Any character stepping in opens it and it closes again when the character leaves. A mob only 'sees' the hero if the hero's cell is in its field of view and the hero is not invisible; once it loses sight, a hunting mob walks to the last known cell and, if still blind there, gives up (shows the lost icon) and returns to wandering. Closed doors also stop bolts (SOLID). <small>`core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:91`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:1215`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1311`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1312`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:290`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1327`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1342`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1343`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1353`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1360`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1400`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1401`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1402`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1403`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1404`</small>
- **Stairs break pursuit.** On a level transition only allies (directable allies, powered allies, intelligent allies within 5 tiles) are carried; enemies are never carried, so taking stairs fully breaks contact. Quest branches: the mine is left through its branch exit; the vault is left with the Escape Crystal. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1686`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1687`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1688`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1690`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1695`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1696`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1699`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1700`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1701`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1702`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1703`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:83`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:85`</small>
- **Large enemies and narrow spaces.** A cell is 'open space' only if it is not solid and has an open corner with both adjacent cells open; doorways and 1-wide corridors are not open space. LARGE mobs cannot path into non-open cells, so a doorway or corridor stops them physically. <small>`core/…/levels/Level.java:889`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:890`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:891`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:892`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:893`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:895`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:896`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:897`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:898`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:899`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:900`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:581`</small>
- **Resistances and immunities.** Each matching resistance halves damage from that source (multiplicative); an immunity zeroes it. Properties carry built-in sets: INORGANIC immune to Bleeding/ToxicGas/Poison; FIERY resists Fireblast and fire elementals and is immune to Burning/Blazing; ICY resists Frost wand/frost elementals and is immune to Frost/Chill; ELECTRIC resists lightning sources; MINIBOSS/BOSS immune to ally-making buffs (corruption etc.) and Dread; STATIC immune to all AI and stun/slow debuffs. <small>`core/…/actors/Char.java:925`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:926`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:927`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:929`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1356`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1357`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1358`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1359`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1365`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1366`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1367`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1368`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1376`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1377`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1378`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1379`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1414`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1415`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1416`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1417`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1421`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1422`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1423`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1424`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1425`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1426`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1429`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1430`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1439`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1440`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1441`</small>
- **EXP gating.** A kill only grants EXP if hero level &lt;= mob.maxLvl; many quest mobs have maxLvl=-2 (never any EXP) and all vault mobs have EXP 0 (vault kills instead advance ring identification). <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:951`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:964`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:966`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:967`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:968`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:969`</small>
- **Initial state.** Mobs default to SLEEPING; mobs added by the respawner start WANDERING; individual classes and quest rooms override this. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:124`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:773`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:774`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:775`</small>
- **Paralysis/terror.** A paralysed mob skips its turn and has defense 0; Terror/Dread force FLEEING. Fleeing mobs that are cornered turn and fight unless still terrified. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:277`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:278`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:279`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:280`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:284`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:285`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1477`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1478`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1479`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1481`</small>
- **Vault stealth patrol facing.** Vault mobs (activateSteathGameplayBehaviour) detect by facing: the angle between their last step and the hero is compared as a raw difference of atan2 degrees, without wrapping at +/-180. For a mob walking roughly due west, a hero slightly above vs slightly below its path can therefore be treated as "behind" (near-zero detection) even though it is in front. A mob that did not just step (previousPos not adjacent) uses the plain 1/(distance+stealth) sleeping-style roll. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1575`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1576`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1577`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1578`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1581`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1582`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1583`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1584`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1585`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1586`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1587`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1588`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1589`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1591`, `SPD-classes/…/utils/PointF.java:150`, `SPD-classes/…/utils/PointF.java:151`</small>

## Crystal guardian {#crystalguardian}

`actors.mobs.CrystalGuardian` · depths quest: Blacksmith crystal mine (branch 1 of depth 12-14); one per large mine room · **Tactic:** Guardians are an obstacle, not a kill target: they cannot die and give nothing.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 100 · accuracy 20 · evasion 14 · damage 10-16 · armour 0-10 · EXP 10 · max level -2 · properties INORGANIC, MINIBOSS. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:46`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:98`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:115`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Normal speed (1) in open space, but in any non-open (enclosed) cell its speed is divided by 4 (min 0.25) i.e. up to 4 turns per tile. Stepping onto a crystal shatters it and costs an extra full move. While HUNTING it will stomp through crystal walls if the walkable path is more than twice the straight distance. Spire can Haste far-away awake guardians (x3). Speed `slow`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:205`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:206`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:207`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:208`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:210`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:214`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:215`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:216`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:217`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:223`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:224`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:229`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:230`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:231`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:232`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:234`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:235`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:236`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:454`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:455`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:775`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `melee`, reach 1. Adjacent only (default Mob.canAttack). Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:558`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:559`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:753`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:94`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:98`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:99`</small>
- **Unkillable - crumples and regenerates.** When HP would hit 0 it is set to 1 and the guardian enters 'recovering' (crumpled): all buffs except Doom/Cripple are removed, and each turn it only heals 5 HP (no moving, no attacking) until full, then stands back up. While recovering it has 0 defense and cannot be surprised; damage from any character other than the hero or the spire is blocked (so allies and other mobs cannot hit it), but hero hits and non-character sources (wands, bombs, traps, gas) still subtract HP, floored at 1, which delays recovery; pinned thrown weapons are dropped. ### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `avoid`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Guardians are an obstacle, not a kill target: they cannot die and give nothing. Before the spire fight, sneak past sleeping guardians or wall them off (they will not wake if they cannot reach you). Pre-mine a narrow approach tunnel to the spire so that when the 3rd pickaxe hit wakes them they crawl at quarter speed through enclosed cells. When one arrives, stand so the spire's spike wave covers its cell (it takes 18-27 plus cripple and knockback) or knock it to 0 HP and keep tapping it while you finish the spire. Killing the spire drops every guardian it can see. To escape, retreat into enclosed tunnels or leave via the mine exit. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:142`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:146`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:154`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:155`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:156`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:205`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:206`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:207`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:208`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:252`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:259`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:131`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:132`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:376`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:378`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:379`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:628`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:629`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:783`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:784`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:785`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:786`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:787`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:788`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:789`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:825`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:826`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:828`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:819`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:534`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:535`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:536`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:537`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:538`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:539`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:540`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:541`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:394`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:395`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:396`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:397`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:953`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:958`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:959`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:961`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:56`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:223`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:231`, `core/…/sprites/CrystalGuardianSprite.java:63`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Do not try to kill it; knock it down for breathing room.** It cannot die (HP reset to 1) and gives no EXP/loot; taking it to 0 buys ~20 turns (100 HP at 5/turn) while it lies at 0 defense. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:154`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:155`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:156`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:164`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:165`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:951`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Keep a downed guardian down by hitting it.** Hero hits on a recovering guardian lower its HP (floor 1) and it only heals 5/turn, so one hit per turn roughly cancels its regen. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:142`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:143`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:146`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:147`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Fight and flee inside enclosed tunnels.** In non-open cells it moves at 1/4 speed, so a narrow mined tunnel lets you out-walk it and get free ranged hits. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:205`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:206`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:207`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:208`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:889`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:890`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:898`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:899`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:900`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Let the spire's spikes hit it.** Spike waves deal 18-27 to guardians (vs 6-15 to you), cripple them 30 turns (half speed) and knock them away from you. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:127`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:128`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:131`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:132`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:141`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:142`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:143`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:144`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:772`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Kill the spire to drop every guardian in its view.** Spire death deals HT damage to every guardian in its field of view, sending them into recovery. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:376`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:377`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:378`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:379`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Leave sleeping guardians alone; sneak by.** They only wake on a detection roll and do not wake if they cannot path to you; beckon/noise does nothing while asleep. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:244`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:245`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:252`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:255`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:256`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:259`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:260`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1244`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1245`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: yes; contact breaks at: enclosed-tunnel, crystal-walls (path &gt; 2x straight line), stairs, out-of-sight. Outrunnable only in enclosed (non-open) space where it takes up to 4 turns per tile; in open cave space it matches hero speed, and a spire-hasted one is faster. Crystal walls block its walking path unless the detour is more than 2x the direct distance, in which case it stomps through them (extra turn per crystal). <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:205`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:206`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:207`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:208`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:214`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:223`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:224`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:229`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:230`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:231`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:234`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:235`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:236`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:454`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:455`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1686`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Tank its 10-16 hits and knock it down in melee, then return to mining; keep one hit per turn on it while downed if needed. |
| Mage | Wand bolts never miss, so chip it down from a tunnel mouth; don't waste charges trying to 'kill' it - downing it is the goal. |
| Rogue | Stealth past sleeping guardians; a surprise hit on a sleeper is guaranteed but it still cannot die. |
| Huntress | Shoot it while it crawls through enclosed tunnels (4 turns per tile, x1.5 thrown accuracy at range); pinned missiles are dropped when it crumples, pick them up. |
| Duelist | Use weapon abilities to down it quickly when it reaches you, then resume pickaxe hits on the spire. |
| Cleric | Guiding Light makes it 0-evasion for the cleric's next hit; use it to down the guardian fast when it arrives. |

### Community notes

- **Tier 1.** “Guardians in the Crystal Caves spawn only at floor generation (one per large room, up to 3 per floor), do not respawn, and start asleep surrounded by crystals — they won't notice the Hero unless there's a clear unobstructed path of sight. If they do notice and pursue, they can break crystals blocking their path at the cost of an extra turn. Community note: Guardians reportedly cannot be killed by any means, so the practical approach is avoidance (don't open a sightline to a sleeping Guardian) rather than combat.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Old_Mine) (version: not stated). Confirmed: 3 large rooms per mine, one guardian each, spawningWeight 0 and the mine respawner only makes wisps, so none respawn; it starts SLEEPING sealed in crystal; it stomps crystals at +1 move while hunting; HP is reset to 1 instead of dying. Nuance: the wake check is a walkable-path check (PathFinder over passable cells), not a line-of-sight check, and damage or the spire's 3rd-hit alarm (CrystalSpire.java:423-460) wakes/aggroes it regardless. <small>`core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:95`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:101`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:103`, `core/…/quest/MineLargeRoom.java:77`, `core/…/quest/MineLargeRoom.java:84`, `core/…/quest/MineLargeRoom.java:88`, `core/…/quest/MineLargeRoom.java:101`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:57`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:58`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:155`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:156`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:201`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:216`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:224`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:231`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:234`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:236`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:255`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:258`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:259`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:260`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:163`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:164`</small>

## Crystal wisp {#crystalwisp}

`actors.mobs.CrystalWisp` · depths quest: Blacksmith crystal mine (branch 1 of depth 12-14); the mine's regular spawn · **Tactic:** Treat it as a turret you have to break line with. Step behind a crystal or let a guardian/ally stand in the line so it must drift adjacent, then melee it (30 HP).

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 30 · accuracy 18 · evasion 16 · damage 5-10 · armour 0-5 · EXP 7 · max level -2 · properties INORGANIC. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:37`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:82`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:87`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1; flies; treats crystal walls (MINE_CRYSTAL) as passable, so it drifts straight through crystal clusters the hero must mine. Speed `normal`; flies: yes; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:48`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:69`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:70`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:71`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `bolt`, reach 99. Adjacent melee, or a light beam when a MAGIC_BOLT line from it reaches you (stops at the first character and at any solid cell - crystal walls and closed doors block it). No cooldown: it beams every turn it has a line. Attack time: 1 turn (beam spends 1). <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:92`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:94`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:97`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:99`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:100`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:102`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:106`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:107`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:110`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:111`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:125`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:126`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:126`</small>
- **Light beam.** Magic hit roll (double accuracy) for 5-10 LightBeam damage; kills log a special message. Beam damage is in the AntiMagic resist list. The beam is applied with enemy.damage() rather than an attack, so your armour DR does not reduce it (only AntiMagic/Arcane Armor do). *Telegraph:* none before firing; beam visual when it fires <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:125`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:130`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:132`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:135`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:136`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:137`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:138`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:615`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:616`, `core/…/glyphs/AntiMagic.java:131`, `core/…/sprites/CrystalWispSprite.java:67`, `core/…/sprites/CrystalWispSprite.java:75`, `core/…/sprites/CrystalWispSprite.java:76`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:390`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:486`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:941`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:942`</small>
- **Immune.** Bleeding, ToxicGas, Poison (INORGANIC) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:50`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1421`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1422`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `sleeping`; flees: never. Default AI. Initial mine population sleeps; respawns arrive wandering (3x slower respawn in the mine). From the spire's 3rd hit onward, non-hunting wisps are beckoned to the spire; spire death blinds wisps in its view for 5 turns. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:124`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:773`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:774`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:775`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:159`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:163`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:164`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:173`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:174`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:175`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:409`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:410`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:411`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:381`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:382`</small>
- **Evasion.** defenseSkill 16; evasive: no. moderate <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:43`</small>
- **Surprise.** Can be surprised: yes. Standard rules; sleeping wisps take guaranteed surprise hits. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1237`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `close`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Treat it as a turret you have to break line with. Step behind a crystal or let a guardian/ally stand in the line so it must drift adjacent, then melee it (30 HP). If you have a clean line and it is asleep, a surprise hit or ranged volley is fine, but don't trade at range for long - its beam uses double accuracy. It cannot be walled off (flies through crystal) and gives no EXP, so kill it only if it's in the way. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:43`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:92`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:94`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:130`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:616`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:628`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:629`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:783`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:784`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:785`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:786`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:787`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:788`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:789`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:825`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:826`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:828`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:819`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:534`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:535`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:536`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:537`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:538`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:539`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:540`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:541`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:394`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:395`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:396`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:397`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:953`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:958`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:959`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:961`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:56`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:223`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:231`</small>

Same speed as the hero and flies through crystals, so crystal mazes do not stop it (but do stop its beam; crystal is not LOS-blocking, so it still sees you). | Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Close to melee using crystal cover; 30 HP falls fast. |
| Mage | Wand bolts never miss; you both need the same line, so fire first. |
| Rogue | Surprise sleepers for a guaranteed hit; otherwise use crystal to deny the beam. |
| Huntress | Thrown weapons at range get x1.5 accuracy vs its 16 defense; kill it before it closes, or duck behind crystal. |
| Duelist | Melee it at corners; its melee accuracy is the plain 18. |
| Cleric | Guiding Light zeroes its evasion for your follow-up; fight at corners. |

### Community notes

- **Tier 1.** “Wisps are the common respawning enemy of the Crystal Caves branch; they function like other spellcasters (shamans, warlocks), can move directly through the crystal terrain while chasing the Hero, but cannot shoot their ranged attack through crystals — making the crystals themselves useful cover to break their line of sight even though they can walk around/through them to reach you eventually.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Old_Mine) (version: not stated). Confirmed: the crystal mine's createMob returns CrystalWisp (3x respawn cooldown); modifyPassable lets it fly through MINE_CRYSTAL; the beam is a MAGIC_BOLT (STOP_SOLID) and crystal is SOLID, so crystal blocks the beam. Crystal is not LOS_BLOCKING, so it still sees you through crystal and will drift through to melee. <small>`core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:159`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:163`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:164`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:173`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:175`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:48`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:69`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:70`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:71`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:92`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:94`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:126`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “General counter to any enemy with a long-range attack: as soon as it spots you, retreat behind a door or around a corner so it loses line of sight and can't use its ranged attack; this applies to Warlocks, Scorpios, Wisps and similar spellcasters/archers throughout these floors.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Applies to crystal wisps: the beam needs a MAGIC_BOLT line, which doors (SOLID+LOS_BLOCKING), walls, crystal and bodies block. Caveat for wisps: they fly through crystal, so crystal cover only denies the beam, not the approach. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:92`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalWisp.java:94`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`</small>

## Crystal spire {#crystalspire}

`actors.mobs.CrystalSpire` · depths quest boss: Blacksmith crystal mine (branch 1 of depth 12-14), centre of the giant mine room · **Tactic:** Prepare first: find the guardians, note where they sleep, and mine a narrow tunnel to the spire (guardians crawl in enclosed cells).

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 300 · accuracy 0 · evasion 0 · damage 1 · armour 0 (no override: only Barkskin adds to it) · EXP 20 · max level 29 · properties BOSS, IMMOVABLE, INORGANIC, STATIC. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:60`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:689`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:709`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:701`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** IMMOVABLE, PASSIVE; never moves. Speed `immobile`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: no; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:72`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:74`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:76`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:79`, `core/…/quest/MineGiantRoom.java:76`</small>
- **Attack.** `none`, reach 7. No normal attack. Spike abilities target the hero's current cell (diamond) or a line of up to 7 cells from the spire toward the hero (stops at non-crystal solid). Only fires while the hero is in its field of view (it tracks an invisible hero). Attack time: ability every ~15 of its turns; cooldown also drops by (damage/10) per pickaxe hit. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:83`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:101`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:104`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:181`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:182`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:186`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:188`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:189`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:191`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:200`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:205`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:247`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:251`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:252`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:253`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:254`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:331`</small>
- **Only a pickaxe hurts it.** Interacting with the pickaxe swings at it (damage from pickaxe level/augment only); every other damage source does 0 and it is immune to all buffs. Hits 1: warning; hit 3: boss fight starts (alarm to guardians/wisps, boss bar, first ability after 1 turn). Pickaxe damage per swing is a tier-2 melee roll, 2-15 at +0 (NormalIntRange, no strength bonus because the spire is passed as the owner), i.e. roughly 33-35 swings at +0, matching the code comment. *Telegraph:* log warning at hit 1, log alert + shake at hit 3 <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:295`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:296`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:297`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:303`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:304`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:308`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:309`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:315`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:316`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:317`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:319`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:320`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:328`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:330`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:390`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:392`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:393`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:398`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:401`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:402`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:404`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:539`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:63`, `core/…/items/KindOfWeapon.java:249`, `core/…/melee/MeleeWeapon.java:251`, `core/…/melee/MeleeWeapon.java:257`, `core/…/melee/MeleeWeapon.java:295`, `core/…/quest/Pickaxe.java:58`</small>
- **Crystal spikes (diamond / line).** 50/50 a diamond centred on the hero (hero cell + orthogonal neighbours) or a line from the spire toward the hero. Below 2/3 HP it adds a second expanding wave, below 1/3 a third; each wave fires on a later spire turn. Spiked cells become crystal walls. Characters on a spiked cell take 6-15 (guardians 18-27 + Cripple 30), and are pushed away (guardians away from the hero, others away from the spire). Wisps are unaffected. Waves already queued keep landing on later turns even if you leave its sight. *Telegraph:* targeted-cell markers on every cell of the next wave; the hero is interrupted; delay = your next action time clamped to 1-3 turns, so you always get at least one action <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:107`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:109`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:111`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:118`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:119`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:124`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:125`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:127`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:128`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:131`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:132`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:134`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:137`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:139`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:141`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:148`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:157`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:158`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:159`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:160`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:173`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:174`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:175`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:194`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:196`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:197`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:202`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:203`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:216`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:220`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:221`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:224`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:225`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:227`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:228`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:234`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:237`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:238`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:247`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:252`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:253`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:261`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:262`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:264`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:265`</small>

- **Immune.** All damage except the Pickaxe; all buffs/debuffs; Blindness <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:295`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:296`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:297`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:303`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:304`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:308`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:309`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:539`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `passive`; flees: never. Neutral and passive; acts after other mobs (actPriority MOB_PRIO-1) which makes guardian baiting consistent. Does nothing until 3 pickaxe hits; afterwards uses abilities only while the hero is in its view. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:69`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:70`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:72`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:74`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:181`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:182`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:183`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:285`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:286`</small>

- **Surprise.** Can be surprised: no. Not attacked normally; only the pickaxe interaction applies damage. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:295`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:296`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:315`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:316`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `close`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Prepare first: find the guardians, note where they sleep, and mine a narrow tunnel to the spire (guardians crawl in enclosed cells). Hit 1-2 are free. From hit 3, alternate: swing the pickaxe, and whenever markers appear, step off every marked cell (diagonal step escapes the first diamond; sidestep off the line). Below 2/3 and 1/3 HP expect 2-3 expanding waves, so move further. Stand so that arriving guardians get caught in spike cells. When the spire breaks, guardians in its view drop and wisps are blinded - walk out. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:101`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:103`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:104`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:181`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:194`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:216`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:220`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:221`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:224`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:247`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:261`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:295`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:296`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:376`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:381`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:205`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:206`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalGuardian.java:207`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Bring the pickaxe and just keep swinging between dodges.** Only Pickaxe damage counts. Each swing also lowers its ability cooldown by damage/10, so fast mining brings the next spike wave sooner: swing, then dodge the markers. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:295`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:296`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:317`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:319`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:328`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:330`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:331`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Step out of every marked cell.** Each wave is telegraphed with targeted-cell markers and you always get at least one action before it lands. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:194`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:196`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:197`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:202`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:203`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:173`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:174`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:175`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Break its line of sight to pause it.** It only starts new abilities while the hero is in its field of view (it ignores invisibility); waves already queued still land. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:104`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:181`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:182`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:107`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:109`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Use its spikes on guardians.** Guardians standing on spiked cells take 18-27 and Cripple, and are pushed away from you. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:131`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:132`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:141`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:142`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:143`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:144`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Mine your way out of spike crystals.** Spiked cells become crystal; mining a crystal also shatters crystals adjacent to it. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:118`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:1365`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:1368`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:1384`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:1389`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:1390`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:1391`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:1392`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: yes; contact breaks at: out-of-sight, stairs. It never moves; getting out of its field of view stops its abilities. <small>`core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:76`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:104`, `core/…/mobs/CrystalSpire.java:181`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Most HP buffer for eating a stray 6-15 spike; keep mining. |
| Mage | Wands do nothing to it; save charges for wisps and to down guardians. |
| Rogue | Invisibility does not stop it: it still tracks an invisible hero. |
| Huntress | Only the pickaxe damages it; use the bow on wisps/guardians that arrive. |

## Fungal core {#fungalcore}

`actors.mobs.FungalCore` · depths quest boss: Blacksmith fungi mine - NOT reachable in v4.0.0 (quest type rolls only 1-2; fungi = 3 is unimplemented) · **Tactic:** Unreachable in normal v4.0.0 play. If it ever appears: clear or avoid adjacent Fungal Sentries' lines, then beat on the core; it does not fight back.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 300 · accuracy 0 · evasion 0 · damage 1 · armour 0 (no override: only Barkskin adds to it) · EXP 20 · max level 29 · properties BOSS, IMMOVABLE. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalCore.java:27`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:689`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:709`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:701`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** PASSIVE; never moves in normal play (IMMOVABLE itself only blocks knockback/Vertigo-type effects, and the class does not override getCloser). Speed `immobile`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: no; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalCore.java:35`, `core/…/mobs/FungalCore.java:37`, `core/…/quest/MineGiantRoom.java:139`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1433`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1434`</small>
- **Attack.** `none`, reach 0. None in normal play: it stays PASSIVE (aggro() never leaves PASSIVE) and defines no attack; only a buff that forces HUNTING, such as Amok, could make it act, swinging for Char's default 1 damage. Attack time: n/a. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalCore.java:27`, `core/…/mobs/FungalCore.java:30`, `core/…/mobs/FungalCore.java:35`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:884`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:886`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:887`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:528`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:529`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:709`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:710`</small>

## Fungal sentry {#fungalsentry}

`actors.mobs.FungalSentry` · depths quest: Blacksmith fungi mine (centre of each large mine room when the quest type is FUNGI) - NOT reachable in v4.0.0 (fungi quest unimplemented) · **Tactic:** Unreachable in normal v4.0.0 play. If present: route around its lines of fire; if it must die, approach along a path with no line until adjacent or attack while invisible; 200 HP makes a slugging match costly against 50...

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 200 · accuracy 50 · evasion 12 · damage 5-10 · armour 0 (no override: only Barkskin adds to it) · EXP 10 · max level -2 · properties IMMOVABLE, MINIBOSS. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:32`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:90`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:70`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:701`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** IMMOVABLE: getCloser/getFurther always fail. Speed `immobile`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: no; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:45`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:60`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:61`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:65`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:66`, `core/…/quest/MineLargeRoom.java:202`, `core/…/quest/MineLargeRoom.java:183`</small>
- **Attack.** `ranged`, reach 99. Adjacent, or any MAGIC_BOLT line to you (bodies and solid cells block). Uses the normal attack roll (not magic) at range, shown as a poison bolt. Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:75`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:76`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:759`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:761`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:762`, `core/…/sprites/FungalSentrySprite.java:60`, `core/…/sprites/FungalSentrySprite.java:61`, `core/…/sprites/FungalSentrySprite.java:63`, `core/…/sprites/FungalSentrySprite.java:64`, `core/…/sprites/FungalSentrySprite.java:78`, `core/…/sprites/FungalSentrySprite.java:81`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>
- **Poison spit.** Very accurate (attackSkill 50) hits for 5-10 and extend Poison by 6 each hit. *Telegraph:* none <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:70`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:71`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:84`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:85`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:90`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:91`</small>
- **Always notices.** Custom waiting state: notices the hero the first turn the hero is in its FOV (no stealth roll), spending that turn on noticing before it starts shooting. *Telegraph:* alert icon <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:43`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:99`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:102`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:104`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:106`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:116`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:117`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:118`</small>
- **Inflicts Poison.** +6 poison per hit (stacking extension) <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:84`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:85`</small>
- **Immune.** ToxicGas, Poison; ally-making buffs and Dread (MINIBOSS) <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:95`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:96`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:46`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1416`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1417`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `wandering`; flees: never. Starts in its custom WANDERING/Waiting state; switches to HUNTING the moment it sees you; can never chase. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:43`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:99`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:104`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:106`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:110`</small>
- **Evasion.** defenseSkill 12; evasive: no.  <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:38`</small>
- **Surprise.** Can be surprised: yes. Only while it has not seen you (e.g. you are invisible or out of its FOV); it never fails to notice a visible hero. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:290`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:104`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:106`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `avoid`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Unreachable in normal v4.0.0 play. If present: route around its lines of fire; if it must die, approach along a path with no line until adjacent or attack while invisible; 200 HP makes a slugging match costly against 50 accuracy and stacking poison. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:37`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:60`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:76`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:84`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:90`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:290`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Stay out of its lines.** It cannot move, so any cell with no MAGIC_BOLT line to it is completely safe. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:60`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:61`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:76`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:78`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Invisibility.** An invisible hero is not 'in FOV' for mobs, so it cannot target you, and your attacks auto-hit. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:290`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:628`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:629`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Cure poison between exposures.** Each hit adds 6 turns of poison. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:84`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:85`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: yes; contact breaks at: out-of-line, out-of-sight. It never moves; stepping out of its line ends the threat. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:60`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:61`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:65`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSentry.java:66`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Only fight it if it blocks the route; poison stacks fast. |
| Mage | Avoid; wand duels vs 200 HP waste charges. |
| Rogue | Cloak invisibility makes it blind to you. |
| Huntress | Trading shots is a losing race (it has 50 accuracy, 200 HP); avoid. |
| Duelist | Avoid or close from a no-line angle. |
| Cleric | Avoid or close from a no-line angle. |

**Open questions.**

- Fungi quest unimplemented (Blacksmith.java:205, :353-354). Code TODO notes it may get healing when out of FOV (FungalSentry.java:81).

## Fungal spinner {#fungalspinner}

`actors.mobs.FungalSpinner` · depths quest: Blacksmith fungi mine regular spawn - NOT reachable in v4.0.0 (fungi quest unimplemented) · **Tactic:** Unreachable in normal v4.0.0 play. If present: pull it onto bare floor before trading blows, avoid stepping into its regrowth patches, and use ranged attacks while it crosses open ground.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 40 · accuracy 22 · evasion 16 · damage 10-20 · armour 0-6 · EXP 7 · max level -2 · properties none · loot MysteryMeat (12.5% base). <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:33`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:65`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:60`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:70`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1. Speed `normal`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:33`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:167`, `core/…/levels/MiningLevel.java:168`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>

Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:61`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:66`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:170`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:172`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:558`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:559`</small>
- **Regrowth web.** Instead of webs it seeds Regrowth on the cell you are moving toward and the two beside it (cooldown 10); Regrowth grows grass/high grass and roots characters standing in it. *Telegraph:* zap animation toward the target cell <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:46`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:47`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:136`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:148`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:151`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:167`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:180`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:190`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:194`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:195`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:196`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:198`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:231`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:232`, `core/…/mobs/Spinner.java:234`, `core/…/blobs/Regrowth.java:49`, `core/…/blobs/Regrowth.java:50`, `core/…/blobs/Regrowth.java:51`, `core/…/blobs/Regrowth.java:62`, `core/…/blobs/Regrowth.java:63`, `core/…/blobs/Regrowth.java:64`, `core/…/blobs/Regrowth.java:65`, `core/…/blobs/Regrowth.java:66`</small>
- **Tall-grass armour.** Counts HIGH_GRASS and FURROWED_GRASS cells in its 3x3 (its own cell included; plain short grass does not count): damage x(8 - count)/10, i.e. 30% less with one, 10% less per extra; with 8 or 9 such cells the result is 0 or negative and it takes no damage at all. *Telegraph:* none <small>`core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:51`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:52`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:53`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:54`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:55`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:59`, `core/…/mobs/FungalSpinner.java:60`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:828`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:829`</small>

- **Evasion.** defenseSkill 12; evasive: no. lower than a normal fire elemental's 20 <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:265`</small>
- **Surprise.** Can be surprised: yes. It spawns with enemySeen=false and a 1-turn delay; placing the 4th candle costs your turn and the elemental arrives at that same time, but the hero acts first on ties (HERO_PRIO 0 &gt; MOB_PRIO -20), so your next attack on it is a surprise (guaranteed) hit; afterwards standard rules. <small>`core/…/quest/CeremonialCandle.java:188`, `core/…/quest/CeremonialCandle.java:189`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:137`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1327`, `core/…/items/Item.java:139`, `core/…/scenes/GameScene.java:1186`, `core/…/scenes/GameScene.java:1189`, `core/…/scenes/GameScene.java:1192`, `core/…/actors/Actor.java:50`, `core/…/actors/Actor.java:52`, `core/…/actors/Actor.java:267`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `close`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Light the candles standing next to the ritual centre so you can open with a surprise hit, ideally on or next to water. Stay in melee (its melee is weak) and every 3-5 turns, when 'charging' and the 3x3 markers appear, step out of the marked square (the 3x3 is centred on a cell adjacent to you, so one step directly away from that centre takes you out), then re-engage. Any frost source (frost potion, frost wand, frost bomb, chill) takes over half its HP per application. Burning ends as soon as you step into water. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:265`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:324`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:326`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:362`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:365`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:388`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:397`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:99`, `core/…/quest/CeremonialCandle.java:189`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:628`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:629`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:783`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:784`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:785`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:786`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:787`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:788`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:789`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:825`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:826`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:828`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:819`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:534`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:535`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:536`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:537`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:538`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:539`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:540`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:541`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:394`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:395`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:396`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:397`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:953`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:958`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:959`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:961`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:56`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:223`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:231`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Step out of the marked 3x3.** The fireball lands exactly on the telegraphed 3x3 and you always get an action first. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:324`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:326`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:327`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:328`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:362`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:363`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Fight standing in water.** Its burst makes much less fire on water and Burning detaches from non-flying characters in water. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:365`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:366`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:99`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:100`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:178`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:179`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Frost/Chill sources.** Each Frost or Chill application deals 30-36 of its 60 HP instead of applying; Wand of Frost applies Chill after its own damage, so each zap adds the 30-36. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:191`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:192`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:193`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:237`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:238`, `core/…/wands/WandOfFrost.java:98`, `core/…/wands/WandOfFrost.java:101`, `core/…/wands/WandOfFrost.java:102`, `core/…/wands/WandOfFrost.java:103`, `core/…/wands/WandOfFrost.java:105`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Melee it.** Accuracy 15 and 10-12 damage make its melee the least dangerous thing it does; low defense 12. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:265`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:388`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:390`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:397`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:399`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Open with a surprise hit.** Guaranteed first hit while it has enemySeen=false. (derived) <small>`core/…/quest/CeremonialCandle.java:189`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:137`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: no; contact breaks at: door, stairs, out-of-sight. Normal speed flier; closed doors block both sight and its STOP_SOLID fireball line. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:300`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Melee through it; step out of each telegraph; water negates the burn. |
| Mage | Wand bolts never miss; a frost-type effect is worth 30-36 damage each. |
| Rogue | Place the last candle while adjacent and open with the guaranteed surprise hit. |
| Huntress | Bow/thrown at range get x1.5 accuracy vs its 12 defense, but remember the fireball line ignores bodies - keep dodging markers. |
| Duelist | Melee; use abilities between fireball telegraphs. |
| Cleric | Melee; Guiding Light removes its evasion for your follow-up. |

**Open questions.**

- Exact hero reach to the ritual centre when the 4th candle lands depends on room layout (RitualSiteRoom not read).

### Community notes

- **Tier 1.** “Fire Elementals burn the Hero out of water, so standing in water snuffs the fire immediately; fighting a Fire Elemental while on water tiles is the recommended defense.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Water part confirmed for the newborn: its fireball seeds only 2 fire on water cells (8 elsewhere) and Burning detaches from a non-flying target in water. Note the newborn's melee never ignites (Elemental.java:406-410); only the fireball burns. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:365`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:366`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:368`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:373`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:99`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:100`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:178`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:179`</small>
- **Tier F.** “<del>(though exposed scrolls in the backpack may still burn on the first hit)</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Item burning needs 4+ consecutive burning turns ((turns-3)/3 chance), so the first hit cannot burn scrolls; water ends Burning after at most one tick. <small>`core/…/buffs/Burning.java:120`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:121`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:122`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “Fire Elementals take heavy bonus damage from the Chill effect” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Fire_elemental) (version: not stated). Each Frost/Chill application is replaced by HT/2-3HT/5 damage (30-36 at 60 HT). <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:191`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:192`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:193`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:194`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:237`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:238`</small>
- **Tier F.** “<del>to the point that a chill/freeze source can one-hit-kill one</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Fire_elemental) (version: not stated). One application deals 30-36 of 60 HP: it cannot one-shot a full-HP elemental; two applications (or one on a hurt elemental) kill. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:71`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:193`</small>
- **Tier F.** “<del>do not use a Wand of Firebolt or a Potion of Liquid Flame on a Fire Elemental, as fire damage does nothing to it and may slightly heal it instead</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Fire_elemental) (version: not stated). FIERY makes it immune to Burning/Blazing (so Liquid Flame fire does nothing) but only resists Wand of Fireblast (half damage, not zero); no code heals it from fire. "Wand of Firebolt" is the pre-rework name. The advice not to use fire is still sound. <small>`core/…/actors/Char.java:1423`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1424`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:235`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “General terrain rule for the three City Elementals: stand on water against Fire Elementals to extinguish burning, but stay off water against Frost and Shock Elementals, since water amplifies their chill duration and their bonus electric damage respectively.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Fire half applies to the newborn (water cuts its fire and ends Burning). The frost/shock halves concern other elementals. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:365`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:366`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:99`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:100`</small>

## Newborn fire elemental (ally) {#elemental-allynewbornelemental}

`actors.mobs.Elemental.AllyNewBornElemental` · depths summoned ally from the Summon Elemental spell (default, un-imbued summon); not a naturally hostile spawn · **Tactic:** Friendly summon: keep it between you and enemies; don't fight next to it on grass since its hits can ignite.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 60 · accuracy `15; \| super.attackSkill(target)` · evasion 12 · damage `Random.NormalIntRange(10, 12); \| super.damageRoll()` · armour 0-5 · EXP 10 · max level 20 · properties FIERY · loot PotionOfLiquidFlame (12.5% base). <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:470`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:388`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:397`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:111`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1; flies. Speed `normal`; flies: yes; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1690`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1699`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1700`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1701`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:354`</small>
- **Attack.** `melee`, reach 1. Adjacent only - rangedCooldown is pinned to Integer.MAX_VALUE, so it never charges a fireball. Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:470`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:473`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:297`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:306`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:308`</small>
- **Burning melee (as summon).** When summoned it uses fire-elemental melee: 50% to ignite a target not standing in water. *Telegraph:* none <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:406`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:407`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:408`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:409`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:242`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:243`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:244`</small>
- **Inflicts Burning.** 50% on melee hit (summoned) <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:243`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:244`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:408`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:409`</small>
- **Immune.** Burning, Blazing (FIERY) <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:235`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1423`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1424`</small>
- **Resists.** Fireblast / fire elemental sources <small>`core/…/actors/Char.java:1423`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `hunting`; flees: never. Summoned next to the hero with an ally buff (InvisAlly, hearts icon). Recasting while one exists on the level teleports it back to you, resets it to HUNTING and does not consume the spell. <small>`core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:72`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:104`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:105`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:106`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:107`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:113`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:114`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:115`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:116`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:117`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:118`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:102`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:103`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:105`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:106`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:107`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:108`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:109`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:121`</small>
- **Evasion.** defenseSkill 12; evasive: no. codex base value; a summon's is overwritten to 5\*regionScale <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:106`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:265`</small>
- **Surprise.** Can be surprised: yes. Not relevant: the hero swaps places with (interacts with) non-enemy mobs instead of attacking them; if an ally is attacked by the hero its defense is 0 anyway. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:880`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:881`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `avoid`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Friendly summon: keep it between you and enemies; don't fight next to it on grass since its hits can ignite. It has no fireball. <small>`core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:113`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:115`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:116`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:470`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:473`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Not an enemy.** It is created as the hero's ally; treat as a friendly unit. Its fire can still ignite grass near you. (derived) <small>`core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:115`, `core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:116`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:243`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:244`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: no; contact breaks at: stairs. Friendly. It is not a DirectableAlly or intelligentAlly, so it stays behind when you change level; recast to summon a new one there. <small>`core/…/spells/SummonElemental.java:115`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Use as a meat shield. |
| Mage | Let it tank while you zap. |
| Rogue | Use it as a distraction for surprise hits. |
| Huntress | Let it tank while you shoot. |
| Duelist | Flank alongside it. |
| Cleric | Buff/heal it as a front-liner. |

**Open questions.**

- No code path found that spawns it hostile; if it ever turned (e.g. via amok) it would be a melee-only fire elemental.

### Community notes

No community claim about this enemy was found.
