    - Bestiary build: empty break-contact list recorded as 'stairs': the summon stays behind on a level change.

## Wraith (corpse dust) {#dustwraith}

`items.quest.CorpseDust.DustWraith` · depths quest: Wandmaker corpse-dust quest (depth 7-9); spawns around the hero while the dust is carried, on any level · **Tactic:** The moment you pick up the dust, walk straight back to the Wandmaker along explored corridors.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 1 · accuracy `10 + level` · evasion 0 · damage `Random.NormalIntRange( 1 + level/2, 2 + level )` · armour 0 (no override: only Barkskin adds to it) · EXP 0 · max level -2 · properties INORGANIC, UNDEAD. Some numbers are set when it spawns; the evasion formula below is the live one. <small>`core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:185`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:81`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:76`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:701`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1; flies. Speed `normal`; flies: yes; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:54`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `melee`, reach 1. Adjacent. Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:76`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:81`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:82`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:558`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:559`</small>
- **Endless spawning.** While the hero carries Corpse Dust, a spawner gains 1 power per turn; the next wraith needs (current wraiths+1)^2 power (capped 49). It appears on a random empty, non-solid cell in the hero's view more than round(viewDistance/3) tiles away (4+ at view 8). If no such cell exists the spawn fails and stored power is capped at 2x wraiths. *Telegraph:* cursed sound; wraith fades in with shadow particles; it acts 2 turns later <small>`core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:73`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:74`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:76`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:100`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:101`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:107`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:108`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:109`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:110`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:120`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:121`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:122`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:125`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:126`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:127`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:128`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:129`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:130`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:134`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:135`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:136`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:137`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:140`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:42`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:154`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:155`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:156`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:157`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:160`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:161`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:166`</small>
- **1 HP, huge evasion.** HT 1; defense is 5x its accuracy (85-95 on depth 7-9), so normal attacks miss a lot but anything that connects kills. *Telegraph:* none <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:49`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:81`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:82`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:85`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:86`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:87`</small>
- **Dies with the dust.** When the dust leaves the inventory (given to the Wandmaker) every DustWraith on the level dies. The dust has no item actions, so it cannot be dropped or thrown: turning it in is the only way to stop the spawner. *Telegraph:* n/a <small>`core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:83`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:84`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:85`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:86`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:148`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:149`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:150`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:151`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:152`, `core/…/npcs/Wandmaker.java:117`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:58`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:59`</small>
- **Immune.** Bleeding, ToxicGas, Poison (INORGANIC) <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:56`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:57`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1421`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1422`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `hunting`; flees: never. Spawns HUNTING. adjustStats (run at spawn and again on every save/load) forces enemySeen=true; a level reset() puts it back to WANDERING. Each wraith's 2nd and 3rd attacks on the hero cost quest score. <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:88`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:97`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:98`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:156`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:190`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:191`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:192`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:193`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:194`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:195`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:69`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:72`</small>
- **Evasion.** defenseSkill 85; evasive: yes. 5\*(10+scalingDepth): 85/90/95 at depth 7/8/9 (codex stores 0 because stats are set at spawn) <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:81`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:82`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:85`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:86`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:87`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:154`</small>
- **Surprise.** Can be surprised: yes. Not on spawn (enemySeen=true), but once it loses sight of you (door, corner) or you are invisible, your next hit is a guaranteed kill. <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:88`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1327`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `chokepoint`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** The moment you pick up the dust, walk straight back to the Wandmaker along explored corridors. Prefer narrow corridors and small rooms so there is no visible cell 4+ tiles away (spawns fail). Kill each wraith as it arrives: wand zaps and surprise hits always connect and 1 damage is lethal; melee swings often miss vs 85+ evasion but a hit kills. - **Escape.** Outrunnable: no; contact breaks at: door, out-of-sight, dispel (give dust away). Same speed and flying. Stairs do not help for long: the spawner is a hero buff and keeps spawning on the new level while you carry the dust. Changing level also resets the spawn cost: only DustWraiths on the current level are counted, so a new level spawns its first wraith as soon as a valid cell is visible. <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:54`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:76`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:90`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:96`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:100`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:108`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:109`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:110`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:111`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:120`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Swings miss often vs 85+ evasion; fight from corridors and let them come one at a time. |
| Mage | Every wand zap is a guaranteed kill - the easiest class for this quest. |
| Rogue | Invisibility makes every hit guaranteed; duck behind doors to re-arm surprise. |
| Huntress | Thrown/bow still roll accuracy (x1.5 at range helps); prefer shooting at wraiths that just lost sight of you. |
| Duelist | Precise Assault accuracy boosts after an ability help land the one hit needed. |
| Cleric | Guiding Light zeroes its evasion for the cleric's next weapon hit - a guaranteed kill. |

### Community notes

- **Tier F.** “<del>Before disturbing a Wraith-spawning tomb, stand with your back to a wall, statue, or room corner — wraiths spawn only in the four cardinal tiles around you, so blocking some of those tiles with terrain caps the number that can appear at once to 3 or fewer.</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Wraith) (version: older version (source flagged oldVersion)). The four-cardinal-tile rule is Wraith.spawnAround (tomb/heap wraiths). Corpse-dust wraiths instead appear on a random empty visible cell more than round(viewDistance/3) tiles away, so standing against a wall does not cap them; standing where no visible cell is 4+ tiles away does. <small>`core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:125`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:126`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:127`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:128`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:129`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:130`, `core/…/quest/CorpseDust.java:135`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:106`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:107`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:108`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “Wraiths have very high evasion, so melee attacks miss often; ... a Wraith only has 1 HP so any connecting hit - including a guaranteed surprise attack performed by retreating behind a door - kills it outright.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/User_blog:108.184.82.95/How_to_kill_Wraiths) (version: older version (source flagged oldVersion)). HT 1 and defense 5x accuracy (85-95 at depth 7-9): misses are common, any hit kills; a hit while it cannot see you is a surprise with defense 0. <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:49`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:82`, `core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:87`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`</small>
- **Tier F.** “<del>they're described as weak to magic-type damage (including fire and poison)</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/User_blog:108.184.82.95/How_to_kill_Wraiths) (version: older version (source flagged oldVersion)). INORGANIC makes wraiths immune to Poison (and ToxicGas/Bleeding). There is no magic or fire weakness in code; magic simply skips the accuracy roll and 1 HP means any damage kills. <small>`core/…/mobs/Wraith.java:57`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1421`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1422`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`</small>

## Greater elemental {#vaultbosselemental}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultBossElemental` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19); boss of the vault's final room · **Tactic:** Bring both a stack of thrown weapons and a melee weapon (and wands if you have them).

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 600 · accuracy 28 · evasion 20 · damage `Random.NormalIntRange( 15, 20 ); \| Random.NormalIntRange( 20, 25 )` · armour 0-10 · EXP 30 · max level 29 · properties none. <small>`core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:81`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:110`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:100`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:115`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1. Speed `normal`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:81`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:84`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `melee`, reach 1. Melee adjacent; special attacks target your cell/direction (see abilities). Every time HP crosses a 120-HP bracket (HT/5) it changes to a different form. A single hit cannot take it below the bottom of the next bracket (excess damage is discarded), so one hit changes form at most once. Changing form halves the environment cooldown, cancels a pending special and forces a fresh one on its next turn (special cooldown set to 0), costs it a turn, strips Chill/Frost on entering fire form and Burning on entering frost form. *Telegraph:* form-colour particle burst; ### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `close`; ranged attacks first: yes.
- **Plan.** Bring both a stack of thrown weapons and a melee weapon (and wands if you have them). Watch its form: Fire -&gt; back off and throw (guaranteed hits at range), when fireball markers appear step directly away from the marked centre, slip through the fire wall's gap. Frost -&gt; go adjacent and melee (+10, easier to hit), sidestep the cone and vortex lines. Shock -&gt; zap with wands or throw; never melee. Every 120 HP it swaps, so re-read the particles/boss bar after big hits. If it goes badly, use the Escape Crystal. <small>`core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:121`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:126`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:127`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:131`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:313`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:337`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:340`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:345`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:354`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:359`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:368`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:375`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:380`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:628`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:629`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:783`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:784`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:785`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:786`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:787`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:788`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:789`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:825`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:826`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:828`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:819`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:534`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:535`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:536`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:537`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:538`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:539`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:540`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:541`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:394`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:395`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:396`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:397`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:953`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:958`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:959`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:961`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:56`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:223`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:231`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Swap weapon type to match its form.** Fire: thrown weapons from range (always hit, +10); Frost: melee (+10, half evasion); Shock: wands/magic (+10 and wand recharge) or thrown (unpunished). The wrong type is quartered. (derived) <small>`core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:121`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:126`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:127`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:131`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:338`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:351`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:358`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:365`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:369`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:379`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:382`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Never melee the shock form.** Melee deals 1/4 and each melee hit zaps you for 5-10. (derived) <small>`core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:313`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:314`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:316`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:380`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:382`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Don't cast at the fire form.** Magic deals 1/4 and shortens its special/environment cooldowns. (derived) <small>`core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:337`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:338`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:340`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:342`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:343`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Read and leave the markers.** Every special is pre-marked with targeted cells and resolves on its next act. Fireball: the 3x3 is centred one step ahead of your last move and always contains you, so step directly away from its centre (reversing your last step works); standing still does not dodge it. Fire wall: walk through the gap. Cone: get outside its 50-degree width or behind solid cover. Bolt: step off the marked line and the four marked ring cells. (derived) <small>`core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:608`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:609`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:637`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:723`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:741`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:871`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:950`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:1211`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:1215`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:868`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:1209`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:1214`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Pace your burst.** Damage taken lowers both of its ability cooldowns (special by dmg/12, environment by dmg/24), and each 120 HP swaps its form. (derived) <small>`core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:393`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:394`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:395`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:386`, `core/…/vault/VaultBossElemental.java:398`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: You can always leave.** The Escape Crystal works anywhere in the vault; boss HP remaining only reduces score. - **Kill or nullify: Paralysis stalls its specials.** A charged special only resolves when paralysed == 0, and new specials are only started when paralysed == 0; BOSS grants no paralysis immunity (freezing is not an option: ICY in frost form, harmful in fire form). | Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Save melee for frost form; carry thrown weapons for fire form; do not melee shock form (quartered + zap-back). |
| Mage | Wands are +10 and recharge in shock form but quartered and speed it up in fire form; carry throwables for fire form, melee the frost form. |
| Rogue | No opening surprise; use invisibility mid-fight for guaranteed hits and to reset its aim. |
| Huntress | Best class here: thrown/bow at range auto-hit fire form for +10 and are unpunished in shock form; switch to melee only in frost form (thrown quartered). |
| Duelist | Weapon abilities in frost form; keep thrown weapons for fire form. |
| Cleric | Cleric spells count as magic: strong in shock form, poor (and accelerating) in fire form. |

**Open questions.**

- Vault content is new in v4.0.0; room geometry for fire wall/chasers assumes the fixed final-room centre (VaultBossElemental.java:668-669, 1283-1284).

No community claim about this enemy was found.

## Modified DM-100 {#vaultdm100}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultDM100` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) · **Tactic:** Watch its patrol, come in from behind or the side, and open with a surprise hit; finish it before it gets a clear line.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 20 · accuracy 25 · evasion 18 · damage 2-8 · armour `super.drRoll() + 4` · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties ELECTRIC, INORGANIC · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultDM100.java:29`, `core/…/vault/VaultDM100.java:44`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:61`, `core/…/vault/VaultDM100.java:49`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1. Speed `normal`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/vault/VaultDM100.java:29`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:40`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `bolt`, reach 99. Melee adjacent, otherwise a lightning bolt whenever a MAGIC_BOLT line reaches you (bodies/solid cells block). No cooldown. Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/DM100.java:76`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:85`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:87`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:88`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:90`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:94`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>
- **Lightning bolt.** Magic roll (double accuracy) for 3-10 LightningBolt damage; resisted by AntiMagic. Bolt damage is applied directly (enemy.damage), so armour DR does not reduce it; AntiMagic does. *Telegraph:* zap animation only <small>`core/…/mobs/DM100.java:97`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:98`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:99`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:100`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:616`, `core/…/glyphs/AntiMagic.java:129`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:390`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:941`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:942`</small>
- **Immune.** Bleeding, ToxicGas, Poison (INORGANIC) <small>`core/…/mobs/DM100.java:57`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1421`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1422`</small>
- **Resists.** Wand of Lightning, Shocking, Potential, Electricity, shocking darts, shock elementals (ELECTRIC) <small>`core/…/mobs/DM100.java:56`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1429`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1430`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `wandering`; flees: never. Vault stealth AI (activateSteathGameplayBehaviour): placed WANDERING on fixed patrol points. Detection while patrolling depends on facing: inside a 75-degree forward cone within 6 tiles 1/(dist/2+stealth) per turn; inside 150 degrees 1/(dist+stealth); behind it 1/(dist+stealth)^3 and zero below 10% (i.e. 0 at 3+ tiles). Being noticed moves it to INVESTIGATING (sprite shows the investigate icon; if unseen the hero gets a one-turn awareness mark), not straight to HUNTING; investigating tracks you while you are in view and a further failed stealth roll (first turn -0.33) promotes it to HUNTING; if it reaches your last known cell without seeing you it gives up and resumes patrol. Sleeping stealth mobs detect at 1/(dist+stealth)^2, zero at ~4+ tiles, and wake into INVESTIGATING. Unseen mobs within 6 tiles emit a coloured ring when they step (red hunting, orange investigating, untinted when patrolling). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultDM100.java:32`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1507`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1508`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1509`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1510`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1511`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1512`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1516`, - **Evasion.** defenseSkill 18; evasive: no.  <small>`core/…/vault/VaultDM100.java:35`</small>
- **Surprise.** Can be surprised: yes. ### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `surprise`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Watch its patrol, come in from behind or the side, and open with a surprise hit; finish it before it gets a clear line. If detected, duck behind a door or corner so it must come to melee. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:47`, `core/…/melee/Dagger.java:66`, `core/…/melee/Dagger.java:67`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:56`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Sneak-attack it on patrol.** Guaranteed hit against only 20 HP (DR 4-8); a surprise hit with depth-17+ gear often kills it before it can shoot. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:47`, `core/…/vault/VaultDM100.java:51`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Break its line.** The bolt is MAGIC_BOLT; a door, wall or body in between forces it to walk. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/DM100.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:88`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Approach from behind its patrol direction.** Detection behind its facing is ~0 beyond 2 tiles. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1591`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1592`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1593`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: no; contact breaks at: door, out-of-sight, escape-crystal. Normal speed; once it loses you it returns to patrol after reaching your last-seen cell. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1422`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1423`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1424`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1425`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:85`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Surprise hit then finish in melee. |
| Mage | Wand bolts never miss; 20 HP falls to a couple of zaps. |
| Rogue | Dagger-type surprise hits roll near max. |
| Huntress | Surprise shot, then more shots; it has only 20 HP. |
| Duelist | Surprise then ability. |
| Cleric | Surprise then Guiding Light follow-up. |

### Community notes

- **Tier 1.** “General counter to any enemy with a long-range attack: as soon as it spots you, retreat behind a door or around a corner so it loses line of sight and can't use its ranged attack; this applies to Warlocks, Scorpios, Wisps and similar spellcasters/archers throughout these floors.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). The DM-100 bolt needs a clear MAGIC_BOLT line; a door or corner forces it to walk. In the vault, losing sight sends it to INVESTIGATING your last cell, not HUNTING. <small>`core/…/mobs/DM100.java:76`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:87`, `core/…/mobs/DM100.java:88`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`</small>

## Modified DM-200 {#vaultdm200}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultDM200` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) · **Tactic:** Open with a surprise hit if you can, then fall back into a doorway or 1-wide corridor.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 80 · accuracy 28 · evasion 15 · damage 10-25 · armour 0-8 · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties INORGANIC, LARGE · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultDM200.java:30`, `core/…/vault/VaultDM200.java:45`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:59`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:69`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1; LARGE: cannot enter doorways, 1-wide corridors or other non-open cells. Speed `normal`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: no; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/DM200.java:53`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:581`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:889`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:890`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `melee`, reach 1. Adjacent melee; gas vent can bend around corners but not pass solid terrain. Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/DM200.java:60`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:130`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:131`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:132`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:134`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:135`</small>
- **Toxic vent.** When it sees you but cannot attack: small chance per turn (~distance/100) to vent, or always vents if it cannot walk closer. Seeds ToxicGas 20 along the path and 100 on your cell; 30-turn cooldown. *Telegraph:* zap animation; gas cloud appears <small>`core/…/mobs/DM200.java:107`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:108`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:117`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:118`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:119`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:121`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:123`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:124`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:126`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:144`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:152`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:153`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:157`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:158`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:159`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:166`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:170`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:171`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:172`</small>
- **Inflicts ToxicGas.** gas cloud damage while you stand in it <small>`core/…/mobs/DM200.java:124`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:126`</small>
- **Immune.** Bleeding, ToxicGas, Poison (INORGANIC) - its own gas does not hurt it <small>`core/…/mobs/DM200.java:52`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1421`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1422`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `wandering`; flees: never. Vault stealth AI (activateSteathGameplayBehaviour): placed WANDERING on fixed patrol points. Detection while patrolling depends on facing: inside a 75-degree forward cone within 6 tiles 1/(dist/2+stealth) per turn; inside 150 degrees 1/(dist+stealth); behind it 1/(dist+stealth)^3 and zero below 10% (i.e. 0 at 3+ tiles). Being noticed moves it to INVESTIGATING (sprite shows the investigate icon; if unseen the hero gets a one-turn awareness mark), not straight to HUNTING; investigating tracks you while you are in view and a further failed stealth roll (first turn -0.33) promotes it to HUNTING; if it reaches your last known cell without seeing you it gives up and resumes patrol. Sleeping stealth mobs detect at 1/(dist+stealth)^2, zero at ~4+ tiles, and wake into INVESTIGATING. Unseen mobs within 6 tiles emit a coloured ring when they step (red hunting, orange investigating, untinted when patrolling). Keeps DM-200 HUNTING (vent logic) because only INVESTIGATING/WANDERING/SLEEPING are swapped. <small>`core/…/vault/VaultDM200.java:33`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:55`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1507`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1508`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1509`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1510`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1511`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1512`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1516`, ### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `chokepoint`; ranged attacks first: yes.
- **Plan.** Open with a surprise hit if you can, then fall back into a doorway or 1-wide corridor. It cannot follow; it will vent gas once (step out of the cloud), then you have ~30 turns to shoot/zap it from the corridor while it stands there. Never fight it in open room space where 10-25 hits land at accuracy 28. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:170`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:231`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Retreat into a doorway or corridor.** LARGE mobs cannot path into non-open cells, so it cannot reach you there. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:581`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:889`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:890`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:891`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:895`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:896`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:897`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:898`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:899`, `core/…/levels/Level.java:900`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Bait the vent, then step out of the cloud.** Unable to reach you it vents (30-turn cooldown); move out of the gas and punish it with ranged attacks during the cooldown. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/DM200.java:119`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:166`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:170`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:171`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:172`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:130`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:131`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Sneak attack first.** Guaranteed opening hit on a patrolling one. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`</small>

Any doorway or corridor physically stops it; expect one gas vent. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:581`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:170`, `core/…/mobs/DM200.java:171`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:85`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Surprise hit, then fight from a doorway so it can't reach; or skip it. |
| Mage | Zap from a corridor; wand bolts never miss. |
| Rogue | Surprise hit and retreat into a corridor. |
| Huntress | Ideal: shoot from a corridor it cannot enter. |
| Duelist | Surprise + ability burst, then corridor. |
| Cleric | Fight from a corridor. |

## Fire elemental (vault) {#vaultelemental-fire}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultElemental.Fire` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) · **Tactic:** Surprise it from behind its patrol, ideally while you stand in or next to water; finish with frost/chill effects if you have them (each ~half its HP).

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 60 · accuracy `25; \| 5 + 5\*regionScale` · evasion 20 · damage `Random.NormalIntRange(20, 25); \| Random.NormalIntRange(5\*regionScale, 5 + 5\*regionScale)` · armour 0-5 · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties FIERY · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultElemental.java:41`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:83`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:111`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1; flies. Speed `normal`; flies: yes; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:77`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `bolt`, reach 99. Melee adjacent; ranged zap only when its cooldown (3-5 turns, ticking only while HUNTING) is spent and a MAGIC_BOLT line reaches you (bodies/solid block). Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:83`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:84`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:85`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:94`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:95`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:115`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:118`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:119`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:120`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:134`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:137`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:141`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:143`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:144`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:145`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:147`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:170`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:182`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>

- **AI.** Starts `wandering`; flees: never. Vault stealth AI (activateSteathGameplayBehaviour): placed WANDERING on fixed patrol points. Detection while patrolling depends on facing: inside a 75-degree forward cone within 6 tiles 1/(dist/2+stealth) per turn; inside 150 degrees 1/(dist+stealth); behind it 1/(dist+stealth)^3 and zero below 10% (i.e. 0 at 3+ tiles). Being noticed moves it to INVESTIGATING (sprite shows the investigate icon; if unseen the hero gets a one-turn awareness mark), not straight to HUNTING; investigating tracks you while you are in view and a further failed stealth roll (first turn -0.33) promotes it to HUNTING; if it reaches your last known cell without seeing you it gives up and resumes patrol. - **Surprise.** Can be surprised: yes. Patrolling and investigating vault mobs clear enemySeen every turn they fail to spot you (Wandering.continueWandering clears it, Investigating reuses it), so a hero attack while they patrol/investigate is a surprise attack: their defense is 0 and the hit is guaranteed. Exception: on the turn a patrol first notices you, noticeEnemy sets enemySeen=true until its next act. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1287`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1602`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `surprise`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Surprise it from behind its patrol, ideally while you stand in or next to water; finish with frost/chill effects if you have them (each ~half its HP). If detected, fight it from water so the burn never sticks. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:191`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:192`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:193`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:237`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:238`, `core/…/vault/VaultElemental.java:41`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:628`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:629`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:783`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:784`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:785`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:786`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:787`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:788`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:789`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:825`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:826`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:828`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:819`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:534`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:535`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:536`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:537`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:538`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:539`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:540`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:541`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:394`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:395`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:396`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:397`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:953`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:958`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:959`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:961`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:56`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:223`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:231`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Surprise it on patrol.** Guaranteed opening hit against its 20 evasion. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`</small>

Normal-speed flier; patrol logic returns it to its route once it loses you. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1422`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1423`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1424`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1425`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:85`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Surprise hit, then melee from a water tile. |
| Mage | Frost-type wand effects deal 30-36 per application. |
| Rogue | Surprise with dagger-type weapon for near-max damage. |
| Huntress | Surprise shot; thrown x1.5 at range. |
| Duelist | Surprise + ability; stand in water. |
| Cleric | Surprise, then Guiding Light follow-up. |

### Community notes

- **Tier 1.** “Fire Elementals burn the Hero out of water, so standing in water snuffs the fire immediately; fighting a Fire Elemental while on water tiles is the recommended defense.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Neither its melee proc nor its zap can ignite a target standing in water, and Burning detaches in water. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:243`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:251`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:252`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:99`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:100`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:178`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:179`</small>
- **Tier F.** “<del>(though exposed scrolls in the backpack may still burn on the first hit)</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Scrolls only burn after 4+ burning turns, and never in the vault level. <small>`core/…/buffs/Burning.java:120`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:121`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:122`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “Fire Elementals take heavy bonus damage from the Chill effect” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Fire_elemental) (version: not stated). Each Frost/Chill application is replaced by HT/2-3HT/5 damage (30-36 at 60 HT). <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:191`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:192`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:193`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:194`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:237`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:238`</small>
- **Tier F.** “<del>to the point that a chill/freeze source can one-hit-kill one</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Fire_elemental) (version: not stated). One application deals 30-36 of 60 HP; it cannot one-shot a full-HP elemental. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:71`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:193`</small>
- **Tier F.** “<del>do not use a Wand of Firebolt or a Potion of Liquid Flame on a Fire Elemental, as fire damage does nothing to it and may slightly heal it instead</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Fire_elemental) (version: not stated). FIERY: immune to Burning/Blazing, only resists (halves) Wand of Fireblast; no healing from fire in code. "Firebolt" is the old wand name. <small>`core/…/actors/Char.java:1423`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1424`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:235`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “General terrain rule for the three City Elementals: stand on water against Fire Elementals to extinguish burning, but stay off water against Frost and Shock Elementals, since water amplifies their chill duration and their bonus electric damage respectively.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Fire half confirmed for this card. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:243`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:251`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:99`, `core/…/buffs/Burning.java:100`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “General counter to any enemy with a long-range attack: as soon as it spots you, retreat behind a door or around a corner so it loses line of sight and can't use its ranged attack; this applies to Warlocks, Scorpios, Wisps and similar spellcasters/archers throughout these floors.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Its zap needs a MAGIC_BOLT line and a spent cooldown; behind a door or corner it must melee. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:137`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:143`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:145`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>

## Frost elemental (vault) {#vaultelemental-frost}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultElemental.Frost` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) · **Tactic:** Surprise it on patrol and never fight it on water. A fire source (liquid flame, fire wand, burning grass) deals ~half its HP per application.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 60 · accuracy `25; \| 5 + 5\*regionScale` · evasion 20 · damage `Random.NormalIntRange(20, 25); \| Random.NormalIntRange(5\*regionScale, 5 + 5\*regionScale)` · armour 0-5 · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties ICY · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultElemental.java:56`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:83`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:111`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Attack.** `bolt`, reach 99. Melee adjacent; ranged zap only when its cooldown (3-5 turns, ticking only while HUNTING) is spent and a MAGIC_BOLT line reaches you (bodies/solid block). Patrolling and investigating vault mobs clear enemySeen every turn they fail to spot you (Wandering.continueWandering clears it, Investigating reuses it), so a hero attack while they patrol/investigate is a surprise attack: their defense is 0 and the hit is guaranteed. Exception: on the turn a patrol first notices you, noticeEnemy sets enemySeen=true until its next act. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1287`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1602`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `surprise`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Surprise it on patrol and never fight it on water. A fire source (liquid flame, fire wand, burning grass) deals ~half its HP per application. If chilled/frozen, back off out of its line until it wears off. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:191`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:192`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:193`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:490`, `core/…/vault/VaultElemental.java:56`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:628`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:629`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:783`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:784`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:785`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:786`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:787`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:788`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:789`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:825`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:826`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:828`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:819`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:534`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:535`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:536`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:537`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:538`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:539`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:540`, `core/…/hero/Hero.java:541`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:394`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:395`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:396`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:397`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:953`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:958`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:959`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:961`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:56`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:223`, `core/…/missiles/MissileWeapon.java:231`</small>

(derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`</small>

- **Kill or nullify: Fire items.** Each Burning application is 30-36 damage. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:193`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:490`</small>

Normal-speed flier; patrol logic returns it to its route once it loses you. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:77`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1422`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1423`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1424`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1425`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:85`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Surprise hit then melee on dry floor. |
| Mage | Fire wand effects deal 30-36 per application. |
| Rogue | Surprise with dagger-type weapon. |
| Huntress | Surprise shot; kite on dry floor. |
| Duelist | Surprise + ability on dry floor. |
| Cleric | Surprise, then Guiding Light follow-up. |

### Community notes

- **Tier 1.** “Frost Elementals chill the target for 3 turns on a ranged hit (5 turns if the target is standing in water) and have a 33% chance to chill on their melee hit as well; because water extends the chill duration, the community strategy is the opposite of the Fire Elemental fight — avoid fighting a Frost Elemental while standing in water.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Ranged proc always freezes; melee proc on 1/3 of hits, or every hit if you stand in water; chill adds 3 turns, 5 on water. Chill at its cap becomes Frost. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:494`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:495`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:496`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:502`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:503`, `core/…/blobs/Freezing.java:75`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “Frost Elementals take heavy bonus damage from fire-based effects, so a Wand of Firebolt, Potion of Liquid Flame, or similar fire damage is the recommended counter.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Burning is in its harmfulBuffs: each application deals 30-36 (HT/2-3HT/5) instead of sticking. "Firebolt" is the old name of Wand of Fireblast. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:490`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:191`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:192`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:193`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:194`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “General terrain rule for the three City Elementals: stand on water against Fire Elementals to extinguish burning, but stay off water against Frost and Shock Elementals, since water amplifies their chill duration and their bonus electric damage respectively.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Frost half confirmed: water makes every melee hit freeze and lengthens chill. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:495`, `core/…/blobs/Freezing.java:75`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “General counter to any enemy with a long-range attack: as soon as it spots you, retreat behind a door or around a corner so it loses line of sight and can't use its ranged attack; this applies to Warlocks, Scorpios, Wisps and similar spellcasters/archers throughout these floors.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Its zap needs a MAGIC_BOLT line and a spent cooldown; behind a door or corner it must melee. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:137`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:143`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:145`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>

## Shock elemental (vault) {#vaultelemental-shock}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultElemental.Shock` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) · **Tactic:** Surprise it on patrol; fight on dry floor with allies spread out. If blinded, back off out of its line until you can see again.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 60 · accuracy `25; \| 5 + 5\*regionScale` · evasion 20 · damage `Random.NormalIntRange(20, 25); \| Random.NormalIntRange(5\*regionScale, 5 + 5\*regionScale)` · armour 0-5 · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties ELECTRIC · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultElemental.java:71`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:83`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:111`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1; flies. Speed `normal`; flies: yes; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:77`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `bolt`, reach 99. Melee adjacent; ranged zap only when its cooldown (3-5 turns, ticking only while HUNTING) is spent and a MAGIC_BOLT line reaches you (bodies/solid block). Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:83`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:84`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:85`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:94`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:95`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:115`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:118`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:119`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:120`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:134`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:137`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:141`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:143`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:144`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:145`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:147`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:170`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:182`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>
- **Elemental melee.** 20-25 melee that arcs 40% of the damage to every character within 2 walkable tiles of the target (chaining on from each, further through water), never the elemental itself; the struck target also takes the arc only if it stands in water. *Telegraph:* none <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:83`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:85`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:520`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:521`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:522`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:523`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:525`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:526`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:529`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:530`, `core/…/enchantments/Shocking.java:89`, `core/…/enchantments/Shocking.java:93`, `core/…/enchantments/Shocking.java:102`</small>
- **Elemental zap.** Blinds you for 5 turns (half of Blindness duration) with a screen flash. Zaps use a magic roll (double accuracy). *Telegraph:* zap animation only <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:550`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:551`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:552`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:553`, `core/…/buffs/Blindness.java:29`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:174`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:616`</small>
- **Opposite element.** No harmful element: it has no harmfulBuffs list, so no item deals bonus damage. *Telegraph:* none <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:203`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:508`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:516`, `core/…/vault/VaultElemental.java:71`</small>
- **Inflicts Blindness.** 5 turns from the zap <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:551`, `core/…/buffs/Blindness.java:29`</small>
- **Inflicts Shocking arc.** 40% of melee damage to nearby characters (and you if in water) <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:523`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:525`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:526`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:530`</small>
- **Resists.** Wand of Lightning, Shocking, Potential, Electricity, shock darts, shock elementals (ELECTRIC); its arc is AntiMagic-resisted <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:516`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1429`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1430`, `core/…/glyphs/AntiMagic.java:123`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `wandering`; flees: never. Vault stealth AI (activateSteathGameplayBehaviour): placed WANDERING on fixed patrol points. Detection while patrolling depends on facing: inside a 75-degree forward cone within 6 tiles 1/(dist/2+stealth) per turn; inside 150 degrees 1/(dist+stealth); behind it 1/(dist+stealth)^3 and zero below 10% (i.e. 0 at 3+ tiles). Being noticed moves it to INVESTIGATING (sprite shows the investigate icon; if unseen the hero gets a one-turn awareness mark), not straight to HUNTING; investigating tracks you while you are in view and a further failed stealth roll (first turn -0.33) promotes it to HUNTING; if it reaches your last known cell without seeing you it gives up and resumes patrol. Sleeping stealth mobs detect at 1/(dist+stealth)^2, zero at ~4+ tiles, and wake into INVESTIGATING. Unseen mobs within 6 tiles emit a coloured ring when they step (red hunting, orange investigating, untinted when patrolling). <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1507`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1508`, ### Community notes

- **Tier 1.** “Shock Elementals are an uncommon elemental variant whose ranged attack blinds the target for 5 turns, and whose melee attack also damages nearby creatures for 40% of the hit and deals 40% extra damage if the target being hit is standing in water; avoid fighting them while standing in water for the same reason as Frost Elementals.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Zap blinds for DURATION/2 = 5 turns; melee arcs 40% to nearby characters, and to the target itself only when it stands in water (the "40% extra"). Rarity (20%) matches Elemental.random / VaultElemental.random (VaultElemental.java:31-37). <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:523`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:525`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:526`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:530`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:551`, `core/…/buffs/Blindness.java:29`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “General terrain rule for the three City Elementals: stand on water against Fire Elementals to extinguish burning, but stay off water against Frost and Shock Elementals, since water amplifies their chill duration and their bonus electric damage respectively.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Shock half confirmed: water adds the 40% arc to you. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:525`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:526`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:530`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “General counter to any enemy with a long-range attack: as soon as it spots you, retreat behind a door or around a corner so it loses line of sight and can't use its ranged attack; this applies to Warlocks, Scorpios, Wisps and similar spellcasters/archers throughout these floors.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Its zap needs a MAGIC_BOLT line and a spent cooldown; behind a door or corner it must melee. <small>`core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:137`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:143`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:145`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>

## Dwarven ghoul (vault) {#vaultghoul}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultGhoul` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) · **Tactic:** Surprise it on patrol and finish it before it trades many 16-22 hits. A solo vault ghoul dies for good; if you see two together, separate them or burst both down within the revive delay.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 45 · accuracy 24 · evasion 20 · damage 16-22 · armour 0-4 · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties UNDEAD · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:31`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:70`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:65`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:75`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1. Speed `normal`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:43`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `melee`, reach 1. Adjacent. Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:65`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:66`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:70`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:71`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:558`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:559`</small>
- **No partner.** partnerID=-2, so it never spawns a partner. The tier-2 rotation places it solo, but the multiple-enemy treasure room places three vault ghouls together (two with maxLvl 0, so they drop no token), which life-link each other. *Telegraph:* n/a <small>`core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:29`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:30`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:35`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:107`, `core/…/levels/VaultLevel.java:533`, `core/…/levels/VaultLevel.java:534`, `core/…/levels/VaultLevel.java:535`, `core/…/levels/VaultLevel.java:536`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:54`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:55`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:59`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:60`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:64`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:79`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:80`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:84`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:85`, `core/…/treasure/VaultMultipleEnemyTreasureRoom.java:89`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1060`</small>
- **Life link.** If another ghoul of its side can see it or is within 3 tiles (distance &lt; 4) when it dies, it collapses instead and revives after 5 x (times downed) turns with 10% HP, unless the host loses sight of it at 4+ tiles (and no other ghoul can take over) or it is over a pit. *Telegraph:* crumpled ghoul sprite stays on the floor <small>`core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:153`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:154`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:155`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:156`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:157`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:158`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:161`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:162`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:252`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:253`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:257`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:258`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:260`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:268`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:289`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:358`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:362`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:363`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:369`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:330`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:331`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:332`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:335`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:336`</small>
- **Shared aggro.** While patrolling/sleeping it walks toward any vault ghoul within 3 tiles that is investigating or hunting. *Telegraph:* none <small>`core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:46`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:47`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:50`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:51`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:52`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:53`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:54`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `wandering`; flees: never. Vault stealth AI (activateSteathGameplayBehaviour): placed WANDERING on fixed patrol points. Detection while patrolling depends on facing: inside a 75-degree forward cone within 6 tiles 1/(dist/2+stealth) per turn; inside 150 degrees 1/(dist+stealth); behind it 1/(dist+stealth)^3 and zero below 10% (i.e. 0 at 3+ tiles). Being noticed moves it to INVESTIGATING (sprite shows the investigate icon; if unseen the hero gets a one-turn awareness mark), not straight to HUNTING; investigating tracks you while you are in view and a further failed stealth roll (first turn -0.33) promotes it to HUNTING; if it reaches your last known cell without seeing you it gives up and resumes patrol. Sleeping stealth mobs detect at 1/(dist+stealth)^2, zero at ~4+ tiles, and wake into INVESTIGATING. Unseen mobs within 6 tiles emit a coloured ring when they step (red hunting, orange investigating, untinted when patrolling). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:34`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1507`, - **Evasion.** defenseSkill 20; evasive: no. uses base ghoul ACC/EVA <small>`core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:49`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:37`</small>
- **Surprise.** Can be surprised: yes. Patrolling and investigating vault mobs clear enemySeen every turn they fail to spot you (Wandering.continueWandering clears it, Investigating reuses it), so a hero attack while they patrol/investigate is a surprise attack: their defense is 0 and the hit is guaranteed. Exception: on the turn a patrol first notices you, noticeEnemy sets enemySeen=true until its next act. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1287`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1602`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `surprise`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Surprise it on patrol and finish it before it trades many 16-22 hits. A solo vault ghoul dies for good; if you see two together, separate them or burst both down within the revive delay. The treasure-room trio life-links: separate them or down all three inside the revive windows. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:66`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:154`, `core/…/vault/VaultGhoul.java:35`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Sneak-attack it.** Guaranteed hit; 45 HP with only 0-4 DR. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:48`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:75`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:76`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: If two ghouls: kill both inside the revive window or drag them apart.** A downed ghoul revives unless no same-side ghoul can see it or is within 4 tiles; a pit kills it permanently. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:154`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:155`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:252`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:253`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:257`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:258`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:259`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:260`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:369`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Respect the damage.** 16-22 at accuracy 24 is a heavy hit for its tier. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:66`, `core/…/mobs/Ghoul.java:71`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: no; contact breaks at: door, out-of-sight, escape-crystal. Normal speed. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1422`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1423`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1424`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1425`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:85`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Surprise then melee; armour matters vs 16-22. |
| Mage | Wand bolts never miss vs its 20 evasion. |
| Rogue | Dagger-type surprise for near-max damage. |
| Huntress | Surprise shot, then kite. |
| Duelist | Surprise + ability burst. |
| Cleric | Guiding Light for a sure follow-up. |

### Community notes

No community claim about this enemy was found.

## Golem (vault) {#vaultgolem}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultGolem` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) · **Tactic:** Stealth around it using its patrol facing. If you must fight: surprise hit, then fight from a corridor with ranged attacks, and step back each time it pulls you out; close a door behind you to end it.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 120 · accuracy 28 · evasion 15 · damage 25-30 · armour 0-12 · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties INORGANIC, LARGE · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultGolem.java:29`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:67`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:62`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:72`</small>

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1; LARGE (no doorways/corridors). Does not self-teleport in the vault: the golem's teleporting Wandering state is replaced by the stealth patrol state. Speed `normal`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: no; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/Golem.java:55`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:57`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:185`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:195`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:196`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:197`, `core/…/vault/VaultGolem.java:28`, `core/…/vault/VaultGolem.java:32`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1516`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1517`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:581`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>

- **Plan.** Stealth around it using its patrol facing. If you must fight: surprise hit, then fight from a corridor with ranged attacks, and step back each time it pulls you out; close a door behind you to end it. Never stand toe-to-toe in the open. <small>`core/…/mobs/Golem.java:63`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:172`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:235`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:239`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Avoid it unless you need the token.** Highest melee in the vault (25-30 at acc 28, DR 0-12, 120 HP) and it grants no EXP. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Golem.java:45`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:63`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:68`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:73`, `core/…/vault/VaultGolem.java:37`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Corridors stop its feet, not its pull.** LARGE blocks doorways/corridors, but when it cannot walk closer it pulls you out (20-turn cooldown) - be ready to retreat again after each pull. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:581`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:235`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:239`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:240`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:172`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Closed doors block the pull.** The pull path is built over non-solid cells and closed doors are solid (and block its view). (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Golem.java:177`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:179`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Sneak attack.** Guaranteed opening hit. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: yes; contact breaks at: door, corridor, out-of-sight, escape-crystal. Corridors stop it but it may pull you back out once per 20 turns while it sees you; a closed door ends both. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:172`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:177`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:212`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:85`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Only melee it with strong armour; otherwise avoid. |
| Mage | Zap from a corridor; wand bolts never miss. |
| Rogue | Best skipped; stealth past it. |
| Huntress | Shoot from a corridor; expect a pull every ~20 turns. |
| Duelist | Avoid or surprise-burst and retreat. |
| Cleric | Avoid; its hits are the vault's heaviest. |

### Community notes

- **Tier 1.** “Golems have the highest attack, HP and armor of the normal Dwarven City enemies” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Golem 120 HP, 25-30 damage, DR 0-12 top the City roster (e.g. Monk 70 HP, Elemental 60 HP / 20-25). <small>`core/…/mobs/Golem.java:45`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:63`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:73`, `core/…/mobs/Monk.java:42`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:71`, `core/…/mobs/Elemental.java:85`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “they can't walk through corridors” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). LARGE: cannot path into non-open cells (doorways, corridors). <small>`core/…/mobs/Golem.java:55`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:580`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:581`</small>
- **Tier F.** “<del>but may teleport to another room while searching for the Hero</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). True for the City golem (Golem.java:195-198), but the vault golem swaps its teleporting Wandering state for the stealth patrol state, so it never self-teleports. <small>`core/…/vault/VaultGolem.java:28`, `core/…/vault/VaultGolem.java:32`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1516`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1517`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “and can also teleport the Hero to a nearby tile if the Hero hides in a doorway, so doorway-camping does not fully neutralize them” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). When it cannot walk closer it pulls you to the free cell beside it farthest from where you stood (20-turn cooldown); kept by the vault golem (only WANDERING/INVESTIGATING/SLEEPING are replaced). <small>`core/…/mobs/Golem.java:147`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:150`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:151`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:152`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:153`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:154`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:164`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:172`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:235`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:239`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:244`</small>
- **Tier 3 (unverified).** “A community-suggested approach is to get a polearm (pole hammer), find a small single-door room, aggro the Golem and retreat into that room, then kill it with overhead reach-attack swings; keep an escape item ready (invisibility potion, scroll of terror, Lloyd's Beacon, or a teleportation seed) in case you get cornered, since Golem hits are heavy.” [source](https://docs.oldmartijntje.nl/Archive/Shattered-Pixel-Dungeon-Strategy-Guide) (version: not stated). Strategy advice, not checkable as a whole. Code caveat: a golem that cannot reach you pulls you next to it every 20 turns while it sees you, so the room plan must include stepping back after each pull; a closed door (SOLID, LOS-blocking) stops both sight and the pull. <small>`core/…/mobs/Golem.java:239`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:244`, `core/…/mobs/Golem.java:172`</small>

## Marsupial rat (vault) {#vaultrat}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultRat` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) - but no v4.0.0 code spawns it (only an unused import in VaultLongRoom) · **Tactic:** Not spawned in v4.0.0. If present, one surprise hit or one normal hit kills it.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 8 · accuracy 24 · evasion 18 · damage 1-4 · armour 0-1 · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties none · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultRat.java:32`, `core/…/vault/VaultRat.java:46`, `core/…/mobs/Rat.java:55`, `core/…/mobs/Rat.java:65`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1. Speed `normal`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/Rat.java:31`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `melee`, reach 1. Adjacent. Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Rat.java:56`, `core/…/vault/VaultRat.java:46`, `core/…/vault/VaultRat.java:47`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:558`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:559`</small>
- **Ratmogrify.** If the hero has the Ratmogrify armor ability and sees it, it turns NEUTRAL. *Telegraph:* none <small>`core/…/mobs/Rat.java:43`, `core/…/mobs/Rat.java:44`, `core/…/mobs/Rat.java:45`, `core/…/mobs/Rat.java:46`, `core/…/mobs/Rat.java:47`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `wandering`; flees: never. Vault stealth AI (activateSteathGameplayBehaviour): placed WANDERING on fixed patrol points. Detection while patrolling depends on facing: inside a 75-degree forward cone within 6 tiles 1/(dist/2+stealth) per turn; inside 150 degrees 1/(dist+stealth); behind it 1/(dist+stealth)^3 and zero below 10% (i.e. 0 at 3+ tiles). Being noticed moves it to INVESTIGATING (sprite shows the investigate icon; if unseen the hero gets a one-turn awareness mark), not straight to HUNTING; investigating tracks you while you are in view and a further failed stealth roll (first turn -0.33) promotes it to HUNTING; if it reaches your last known cell without seeing you it gives up and resumes patrol. Sleeping stealth mobs detect at 1/(dist+stealth)^2, zero at ~4+ tiles, and wake into INVESTIGATING. Unseen mobs within 6 tiles emit a coloured ring when they step (red hunting, orange investigating, untinted when patrolling). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultRat.java:35`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1507`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1508`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1509`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1510`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1511`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1512`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1516`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1517`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1522`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1523`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1549`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1550`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1551`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1552`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1575`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1577`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1578`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1583`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1584`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1585`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1587`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1588`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1591`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1592`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1593`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1601`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1604`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1606`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1609`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1610`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1616`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1653`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1654`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1655`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1657`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1670`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1671`,
