`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:736`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:737`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:738`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:740`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1286`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1291`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1417`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1422`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1423`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1424`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1425`, `core/…/vault/VaultHallwayRoom.java:82`, `core/…/vault/VaultHallwayRoom.java:83`, `core/…/vault/VaultHallwayRoom.java:84`, `core/…/vault/VaultHallwayRoom.java:92`, `core/…/vault/VaultHallwayRoom.java:93`, `core/…/wands/WandOfBlastWave.java:280`, `core/…/wands/WandOfBlastWave.java:281`</small>
- **Evasion.** defenseSkill 18; evasive: no.  <small>`core/…/vault/VaultRat.java:37`</small>
- **Surprise.** Can be surprised: yes. Patrolling and investigating vault mobs clear enemySeen every turn they fail to spot you (Wandering.continueWandering clears it, Investigating reuses it), so a hero attack while they patrol/investigate is a surprise attack: their defense is 0 and the hit is guaranteed. Exception: on the turn a patrol first notices you, noticeEnemy sets enemySeen=true until its next act. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1287`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1602`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `close`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Not spawned in v4.0.0. If present, one surprise hit or one normal hit kills it. <small>`core/…/mobs/Rat.java:36`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Any hit.** 8 HP. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Rat.java:36`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: no; contact breaks at: door, out-of-sight. Normal speed; trivial threat. <small>`core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | One hit. |
| Mage | One zap. |
| Rogue | One hit. |
| Huntress | One hit. |
| Duelist | One hit. |
| Cleric | One hit. |

**Open questions.**

- Spawn path: grep finds VaultRat only as an import in VaultLongRoom.java:25 and it is absent from VaultLevel tiers (VaultLevel.java:528-543).

### Community notes

- **Tier 3 (unverified).** “Don't waste resources on a lone Marsupial Rat unless threatened; save consumables for deeper floors. They're only dangerous in numbers, so if a group is chasing you, retreat to a doorway/corridor and kill them one at a time.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Marsupial_rat) (version: older version (source flagged oldVersion)). Resource/positioning advice is not checkable from code; code confirms a rat is weak (8 HP, 1-4 damage). The vault rat is never spawned in v4.0.0. <small>`core/…/mobs/Rat.java:36`, `core/…/mobs/Rat.java:56`</small>

## Captured shaman {#vaultshaman}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultShaman` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) · **Tactic:** Sneak up from behind its patrol and open with a surprise hit; kill it in the next turn or two.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 35 · accuracy 25 · evasion 18 · damage 5-25 · armour `super.drRoll() + 5` · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties none · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:36`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:51`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:83`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:89`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1. Speed `normal`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:44`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `bolt`, reach 99. Melee adjacent, otherwise an earthen bolt whenever a MAGIC_BOLT line reaches you (bodies/solid block). No cooldown. Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:73`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:74`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:75`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:91`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:94`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:96`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:100`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:104`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:114`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>
- **Earthen bolt + hidden debuff.** Magic roll (double accuracy) for 6-15; 50% of hits also apply its fixed debuff: Weakness 20 (2/5), Vulnerable 20 (2/5) or Hex 30 (1/5). - **Brute-strength melee.** Vault shamans hit 5-25 in melee (buffed to brute level). *Telegraph:* none <small>`core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:83`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:84`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:85`</small>

Exception: on the turn a patrol first notices you, noticeEnemy sets enemySeen=true until its next act. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1287`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1602`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `surprise`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Sneak up from behind its patrol and open with a surprise hit; kill it in the next turn or two. If spotted at range, break the line (corner/door) and make it come to you, then burst it; its melee is strong, so don't trade for long. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:47`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:118`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:85`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:91`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Surprise hit, then finish fast.** 35 HP; the guaranteed opener removes a big chunk before it can bolt. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:47`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Break its line or close in.** Bolt needs a MAGIC_BOLT line; approach via corners. Its melee is heavy too (5-25), so don't linger either way. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:75`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:94`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:85`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Anti-magic armour.** EarthenBolt is AntiMagic-resisted. (derived) <small>`core/…/glyphs/AntiMagic.java:130`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: no; contact breaks at: door, out-of-sight, escape-crystal. Normal speed; a door stops both sight and bolts. <small>`core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:85`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Surprise then melee; beware 5-25 hits. |
| Mage | Wand bolts never miss; 35 HP + DR 5-11. |
| Rogue | Dagger-type surprise near max. |
| Huntress | Surprise shot, then more shots from range; its bolt rolls double accuracy, so finish it fast or break its line. |
| Duelist | Surprise + ability burst. |
| Cleric | Guiding Light sure follow-up. |

### Community notes

- **Tier 1.** “If a Gnoll Shaman spots you at range, break line of sight behind cover immediately to dodge its lightning bolt; retreating behind a door will make it chase into melee range” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Gnoll_shaman) (version: older version (source flagged oldVersion)). The bolt needs a MAGIC_BOLT line; behind cover it must approach. Current code calls it an earthen bolt (Shaman.java:111), not lightning. <small>`core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:73`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:74`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:75`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:93`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:94`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:96`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`</small>
- **Tier F.** “<del>which is the safer place to finish it off</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Gnoll_shaman) (version: older version (source flagged oldVersion)). For the vault variant melee is not safer: its melee is buffed to 5-25 (brute level), versus a 6-15 bolt. <small>`core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:83`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:84`, `core/…/vault/VaultShaman.java:85`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:125`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “General counter to any enemy with a long-range attack: as soon as it spots you, retreat behind a door or around a corner so it loses line of sight and can't use its ranged attack; this applies to Warlocks, Scorpios, Wisps and similar spellcasters/archers throughout these floors.” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Shattered_Pixel_Dungeon/Enemies) (version: not stated). Breaking line works; but in the vault its melee (5-25) is as dangerous as the bolt. <small>`core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:73`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:74`, `core/…/mobs/Shaman.java:75`, `core/…/mechanics/Ballistica.java:49`, `core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`</small>

## Dwarven skeleton {#vaultskeleton}

`actors.mobs.quest.vault.VaultSkeleton` · depths quest: Ambitious Imp's dwarven vault (branch 1, entered from depth 17-19) · **Tactic:** Surprise it on patrol, then either finish with a thrown weapon/wand from 2+ tiles or take the 6-12 burst (double DR) if finishing in melee.

**Stats** (from the [Codex](../codex/mobs.md)): HT 25 · accuracy 24 · evasion 20 · damage 2-10 · armour `super.drRoll() + 4` · EXP 0 · max level 30 · properties INORGANIC, UNDEAD · loot DwarfToken (100% base). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultSkeleton.java:30`, `core/…/vault/VaultSkeleton.java:45`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:66`, `core/…/vault/VaultSkeleton.java:60`</small>

### Mechanics

Tier 1: read from the code at the pinned tag.

- **Movement.** Base speed 1. Speed `normal`; flies: no; amphibious: no; water-bound: no; opens doors: yes; follows you on the stairs: no. <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:47`, `core/…/scenes/InterlevelScene.java:678`</small>
- **Attack.** `melee`, reach 1. Adjacent. Attack time: 1 turn. <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:67`, `core/…/vault/VaultSkeleton.java:45`, `core/…/vault/VaultSkeleton.java:46`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:558`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:559`</small>
- **Bone explosion.** On death (except by falling) every adjacent living character takes 6-12, with the victim's DR applied twice. *Telegraph:* none (bones sound on death) <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:71`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:75`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:79`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:80`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:81`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:125`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:126`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:133`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:134`</small>
- **Inflicts damage.** 6-12 death burst to adjacent characters <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:81`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:125`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:126`</small>
- **Immune.** Bleeding, ToxicGas, Poison (INORGANIC) <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:62`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1421`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:1422`</small>
- **AI.** Starts `wandering`; flees: never. Vault stealth AI (activateSteathGameplayBehaviour): placed WANDERING on fixed patrol points. Detection while patrolling depends on facing: inside a 75-degree forward cone within 6 tiles 1/(dist/2+stealth) per turn; inside 150 degrees 1/(dist+stealth); behind it 1/(dist+stealth)^3 and zero below 10% (i.e. 0 at 3+ tiles). Being noticed moves it to INVESTIGATING (sprite shows the investigate icon; if unseen the hero gets a one-turn awareness mark), not straight to HUNTING; investigating tracks you while you are in view and a further failed stealth roll (first turn -0.33) promotes it to HUNTING; if it reaches your last known cell without seeing you it gives up and resumes patrol. Sleeping stealth mobs detect at 1/(dist+stealth)^2, zero at ~4+ tiles, and wake into INVESTIGATING. Unseen mobs within 6 tiles emit a coloured ring when they step (red hunting, orange investigating, untinted when patrolling). <small>`core/…/vault/VaultSkeleton.java:33`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1507`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1508`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1509`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1510`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1511`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1512`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1516`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1552`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1575`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1577`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1578`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1583`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1584`, `core/…/wands/WandOfBlastWave.java:281`</small>

Exception: on the turn a patrol first notices you, noticeEnemy sets enemySeen=true until its next act. <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1287`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1602`</small>

### Tactics

Derived from the mechanics above; each line names the code it rests on.

- **Approach:** `surprise`; ranged attacks first: no.
- **Plan.** Surprise it on patrol, then either finish with a thrown weapon/wand from 2+ tiles or take the 6-12 burst (double DR) if finishing in melee. <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:125`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:56`, `core/…/wands/WandOfMagicMissile.java:62`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Finish it from range.** The death burst only hits adjacent characters. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:79`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:80`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: If you must finish in melee, rely on armour.** Your DR is applied twice against the burst. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:125`</small>
- **Kill or nullify: Surprise hit.** Guaranteed opener vs 25 HP. (derived) <small>`core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1297`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1298`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1412`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:1430`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:873`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:874`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:875`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:876`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:796`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:797`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:798`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:799`, `core/…/mobs/Mob.java:801`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:662`, `core/…/actors/Char.java:678`</small>
- **Escape.** Outrunnable: no; contact breaks at: door, out-of-sight, escape-crystal. Normal speed. <small>`core/…/levels/Terrain.java:90`, `core/…/quest/EscapeCrystal.java:85`</small>

| Class | Note |
|---|---|
| Warrior | Armour doubles against the burst; melee is fine. |
| Mage | Finish with a wand from range. |
| Rogue | Surprise with dagger-type weapon; step back before the kill if possible. |
| Huntress | Finish from range: no burst. |
| Duelist | Surprise + ability; accept the burst or finish with thrown. |
| Cleric | Finish from range or eat the burst. |

### Community notes

- **Tier F.** “<del>Skeletons explode on death for damage equal to their normal melee hit, but armor absorption against that explosion is halved compared to normal hits</del>” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Skeleton) (version: older version (source flagged oldVersion)). The burst is its own 6-12 roll (melee is 2-10), and your DR is applied twice against it (armour is doubled, not halved). <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:67`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:81`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:124`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:125`</small>
- **Tier 1.** “finish Skeletons off with a ranged attack from a distance once they're at very low HP so the explosion can't reach you” [source](https://pixeldungeon.fandom.com/wiki/Skeleton) (version: older version (source flagged oldVersion)). Only adjacent characters (NEIGHBOURS8) are hit; a kill from 2+ tiles avoids it. Death by falling (Chasm) skips the burst. <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:75`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:78`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:79`, `core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:80`</small>
- **Tier 3 (unverified).** “Aim to have armor that blocks roughly 4+ damage on average before descending into Prison, specifically to survive the death-explosion reliably if you can't kill a Skeleton before it reaches melee range.” [source](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=TE1zJ1Eh7fg) (version: not stated). Gear-threshold advice for Prison; not checkable. Code does show armour counts double against the burst; at vault depth the unscaled 6-12 burst is minor. <small>`core/…/mobs/Skeleton.java:125`</small>
